### [71] [Loss of positive definiteness is a symptom, not the cause, of high-Weissenberg-number breakdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15334)
*Yuan Yu,Lanjin Lian,Feiyang Chu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了高Wi数下构型张量失去正定性并非数值崩溃的充分必要条件，溶剂粘度比β和耦合方案的选择才是决定性因素；提出了面向不定态的定量理论（增长率有界、方向分辨不稳定阈值λ_min(A)<-β/(1-β)），证明了违反会以λ/2时间尺度自愈，并给出了稳定的耦合方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高Weissenberg数下数值模拟常因构型张量失去对称正定性（SPD）而崩溃，但传统理论基于无溶剂粘度的Maxwell模型，无法解释含溶剂粘度（β>0）时的行为，缺乏对不定态的定量分析及其计算后果的系统研究。

Method: 采用冻结系数分析推导增长率与不稳定阈值，利用行列式恒等式证明自愈性质；通过谱方法和格子Boltzmann方法进行数值验证；在四辊流动中比较多种耦合方案（含局部二阶矩应力源和散度耦合变体）。

Result: 1) 增长率在所有波数上一致有界，方向分辨阈值λ_min(A)<-β/(1-β)；2) 溶剂粘度趋于零时恢复经典σ∝k灾变；3) 违反自愈时间尺度λ/2，持续违反衡量数值误差；4) 强制SPD延迟崩溃15个对流时间但降低Wi数30%；5) 局部二阶矩源在Wi=50且detA≈-8.5×10⁵时稳定，散度耦合在t*=47失败；6) 稳定方案在Wi=10和20下匹配基准0.05%和0.18%。

Conclusion: 失去正定性既非必要也非充分条件导致崩溃，离散耦合路线起决定性作用；违反正定性可作为分辨率指标，并提供运行时监测工具。

Abstract: Numerical breakdown at high Weissenberg number is often attributed to loss of symmetric positive definiteness (SPD) of the conformation tensor. That conclusion follows from Maxwell-type models without solvent viscosity. With solvent fraction $β>0$, the initial-value problem is locally well posed for arbitrary symmetric stress. We derive the missing quantitative theory for indefinite states and test its computational consequences. Frozen-coefficient analysis gives a growth rate uniformly bounded in wavenumber and the direction-resolved instability threshold $λ_{\min}(A)<-β/(1-β)$; stress diffusion supplies a closed-form cutoff, while the classical $σ\propto k$ catastrophe is recovered as solvent viscosity vanishes. A determinant identity shows that violations self-heal on the timescale $λ/2$, so persistent violations measure the truncation error that recreates them. Spectral and lattice Boltzmann tests reproduce the threshold, solvent-fraction reversal, and resolution independence. In four-roll-mill interventions, enforcing SPD delays blow-up by 15 convective times but reduces the stagnation-point Weissenberg number by 30%. Across five coupling schemes, a local second-moment stress source remains stable through the full budget at $\mathrm{Wi}=50$ while carrying $\det A\approx-8.5\times10^5$; the divergence-coupled variant fails at $t^*=47$. The surviving scheme matches published benchmarks within 0.05% and 0.18% at $\mathrm{Wi}=10$ and 20. Thus loss of positive definiteness is neither necessary nor sufficient for breakdown: the discrete coupling route decides, and the violation is a resolution gauge for which we provide run-time monitors.

</details>


### [72] [Atomic Design Transformer: Scaffold-Conditioned 3D Molecule Generation via xTB-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15918)
*Takao Kotani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了原子设计变换器（ADT），一种SE(3)不变的3D分子生成模型，通过自回归方式逐个放置原子，并利用强化学习（RLVR）将拓扑保持率从~54%提升至~98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D分子生成方法难以兼顾SE(3)不变性、自回归生成效率和化学有效性，尤其在大型分子中离散化误差累积导致拓扑结构破坏。作者希望实现一种直接、高效且拓扑保持的3D分子生成方法。

Method: 1) ADT采用自回归方式逐个放置原子，通过将新原子位置编码在已放置原子的局部坐标系中实现SE(3)不变性；2) 使用普通因果Transformer作为骨干网络；3) token流同时指定3D结构和化学键图G，无需键序分配；4) 引入xTB拓扑保持率（XTP）评估生成分子的拓扑保真度；5) 使用基于可验证xTB奖励的强化学习（RLVR）微调模型；6) 提出逆运动学变换器（IKT）解决大分子的离散化误差问题。

Result: 预训练模型在GEOM-Drugs≤30重原子数据集上XTP约54%，有效分子产率约50%。经RLVR微调后，XTP提升至约98%，有效分子产率提升至约95%，同时保持原始数据集的尺寸和组成分布。

Conclusion: ADT能够实现直接的3D分子生成，RLVR显著提升了生成分子的拓扑保持率和化学有效性，IKT进一步解决了大分子的离散化误差问题。

Abstract: We present an SE(3)-invariant transformer for 3D-molecule generation,
  the Atomic Design Transformer (ADT). ADT places atoms one
  at a time, autoregressively. SE(3) invariance is achieved by
  tokenization: each new atom's position is encoded in the local
  coordinate frame of a previously placed atom. The backbone is a plain
  causal transformer. The token stream fully specifies a 3D structure
  together with its chemical-bond graph G, without any bond-order
  assignment. To score generated molecules we introduce the xTB
  topology-preservation rate (XTP): the fraction of molecules for
  which an xTB GFN2 relaxation preserves G specified by the token stream.
  For XTP-accepted molecules we also report the
  relaxation energy and the root mean square of the atomic displacement (RMSD). We evaluate
  two ADT models. The first is ADT pretrained on the GEOM-Drugs
  $\le\!30$-heavy-atom dataset; we benchmark scaffold-conditioned 3D
  generation across seven drug-like scaffolds from the model. It
  reaches an XTP of ${\sim}54\%$ and a valid-molecule yield
  $N^{\mathrm{gen}}/N$ of ${\sim}50\%$, where $N^{\mathrm{gen}}/N$ is the
  fraction of samples that are distinct, topology-preserving, and
  chemically valid. The second model continues from the first by
  reinforcement learning against the verifiable xTB reward (RLVR), using
  no external molecules. RLVR raises XTP to ${\sim}98\%$ and
  $N^{\mathrm{gen}}/N$ to ${\sim}95\%$, while approximately preserving the
  GEOM-Drugs size and composition distributions. Finally, we present
  an Inverse-Kinematics Transformer that recovers XTP for large molecules, where discretization error accumulates. ADT thus enables direct 3D generation.

</details>


### [73] [CADAQUES: A Cost-Aware Dual Architecture for Query-Efficient Autonomous Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16127)
*Jorge Bravo-Abad*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: CADAQUES 是一个开源 Python 框架，将成本（墙钟时间、CPU 小时、货币成本、LLM tokens）作为自主发现循环的一等公民，通过分离 Oracle（回答查询）和 Driver（提出查询）两个协议，并使用向量预算和不可变账本追踪每次交易的成本声明与结算，实现对不同成本查询和决策开销的精细化管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主发现系统框架继承数值优化控制结构，存在三个问题：实验运行固定迭代次数、查询成本不在编程接口中体现、决策被视为免费资源。然而实际中查询成本可相差数个数量级，且基于大语言模型或昂贵代理模型的规划器自身也消耗大量资源，因此需要将成本显式纳入自主发现循环的核心设计。

Method: 提出两个结构化协议：Oracle（回答查询）和 Driver（提出查询）；引入向量预算（包含墙钟时间、CPU 小时、货币成本、LLM tokens）；使用追加型不可变账本记录每次交易执行前声明的成本和执行后结算的成本，使两者差异成为可观测的闭环属性。

Result: 以二维 Ising 模型临界温度定位为测试用例，在存在噪声的有限尺寸估计场景中：专注于最优观测结果的策略会被噪声诱导的峰值误导；而采用先使用廉价低保真查询探索、再用高保真查询精炼的策略，在设定预算下误差更低且方差更小。计量开销每次仅增加数十微秒，比最便宜的 Oracle 查询低三个数量级。

Conclusion: CADAQUES 通过将成本作为设计原语，使得资源感知的发现策略能够在不同保真度查询之间灵活调度，在噪声场景下优于传统固定高保真策略，且框架开销极小，具有实际可用性。

Abstract: Autonomous discovery systems couple a resource that answers queries (a simulator, instrument, or analytic model) to an algorithm that selects what to query next. Most software frameworks for this loop inherit the control structure of numerical optimization: campaigns run for a fixed number of iterations, query costs are absent from the programming interface, and decision-making is treated as free. In practice, queries may differ in cost by orders of magnitude, and planners built on large language models or expensive surrogates consume resources of their own. Here we present CADAQUES, an open-source Python framework built on one architectural principle: cost is a first-class primitive of the discovery loop. CADAQUES separates the loop into two structural protocols, an Oracle that answers queries and a Driver that proposes them, and charges both evaluations and decisions against a common vector-valued budget spanning wall time, CPU hours, monetary cost, and language model tokens. An append-only ledger records, for each transaction, the cost declared before execution and the cost settled afterwards, making their discrepancy an observable property of the campaign. We evaluate the architecture by locating the critical temperature of the two-dimensional Ising model from noisy finite-size estimates against the exact thermodynamic-limit reference. In this noisy setting, strategies that concentrate around the best observed result can be misled by noise-induced peaks, whereas a schedule that explores with cheap low-fidelity queries and refines with higher-fidelity ones yields lower and less variable errors than high fidelity throughout, at the studied budget scale. Metering adds tens of microseconds per iteration, three orders of magnitude below the cheapest oracle query. The framework is MIT-licensed and archived at Zenodo (doi:10.5281/zenodo.21293589).

</details>

### [74] [Shielded RL for Route-Charged Parity-Term Ordering in QEDA Phase Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15307)
*Owen Friedewald,Ali Shiri Sichani,Chi-Ren Shyu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将QEDA相位组件中的项排序问题建模为带屏蔽的强化学习问题，使用精英策略（交叉熵方法）训练排序策略，在IBM类重六角硬件映射下，相比默认顺序降低CX门数量22.3%，相比2-opt和模拟退火降低5.7-12.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QEDA布局电路中，相位项的交换顺序在逻辑上等价，但经硬件映射后，顺序不同会导致CNOT抵消、交互局部性和路由压力的显著差异，从而影响路由代价。现有启发式方法无法充分优化这一排序问题。

Method: 将项排序问题建模为带屏蔽的强化学习问题：可行性屏蔽确保每一步只能选择未发射的项，保证轨迹为有效排列；采用交叉熵方法训练精英策略；代理奖励函数结合支持转移大小和重六角拓扑距离特征；使用Qiskit路由在合成IBM风格重六角映射上验证。

Result: 在36项奇偶游走组件（50项种子×2转译器种子）上，学习到的排序使平均路由CX降至336.0，相比2-opt和模拟退火降低5.7-12.2%，相比默认构造顺序降低22.3%；路由CX和路由深度增益经Bonferroni校正后显著。诚实迁移审计表明代理奖励对奇偶游走组件有预测力，但对提取密集型或token/置换电路不适用。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的项排序方法能有效优化QEDA相位组件在硬件映射后的路由代价，但代理奖励的设计需针对具体电路类型进行调整，其适用范围限于奇偶游走类组件。

Abstract: Commuting phase terms in quantum electronic design automation (QEDA) placement circuits are logically invariant under reordering, yet their routed cost varies substantially after hardware mapping, since term order affects CNOT cancellation, interaction locality, and routing pressure. We cast parity/support phase-term ordering within a QEDA phase component as a shielded reinforcement-learning problem: a feasibility shield restricts each step to unemitted terms, so every trajectory is a valid permutation by construction, and an elite (cross-entropy-method) policy is trained against a route-charged proxy combining support-transition size and heavy-hex topology-distance features. We validate by direct Qiskit routing of logically equivalent circuits to a synthetic IBM-style heavy-hex map. On 36-term parity-walk components (50 term seeds x 2 transpiler seeds, statistics at the term-seed level), the per-instance learned ordering reduces mean routed CX to 336.0, a 5.7-12.2% paired reduction over 2-opt and simulated-annealing search at equal or greater proxy budget and 22.3% over the default construction order; routed-CX and routed-depth gains are significant after Bonferroni correction. Honest transfer audits show the proxy is predictive for the parity-walk component but not for extraction-heavy or token/permutation circuits, which require architecture-aware rewards, scoping the contribution accordingly.

</details>


### [75] [Structure-Agnostic Unitary Learning from Quantum Observable Dynamics with Application to Hamiltonian Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15316)
*Mohamed Berkani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种变分算法，通过时间序列可观测量匹配来学习未知量子酉算子，无需对目标做任何结构假设。核心思想是用硬件高效参数化电路学习演化算子U，再通过经典后处理（矩阵对数）识别哈密顿量。三组实验验证了方法的通用性：无噪声下精确梯度训练达到MSE 1.61e-14并恢复六位精度的哈密顿系数；门学习实验（CNOT、iSWAP、Haar随机SU(4)）均达到过程保真度1.000000，证明不依赖Trotter化结构；量子部署实验（Qiskit Aer退极化噪声）恢复所有三项Ising哈密顿项，误差低于8%。优化器SPSA-Adam结合了SPSA的两点梯度估计与Adam的自适应矩更新，采用四阶段矩预热课程将全局非凸问题转化为局部良态问题序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子过程层析和哈密顿量学习通常需要大量资源或对目标有先验结构假设（如已知哈密顿量形式、Trotter化结构等），缺乏一种无需结构假设、资源高效的通用量子酉算子学习方法。

Method: 1) 用硬件高效参数化电路近似目标酉算子U，通过可观测量时间序列匹配作为损失函数进行变分优化；2) 学习到U后，通过矩阵对数经典后处理提取哈密顿量；3) 提出SPSA-Adam优化器，结合SPSA的两点梯度估计与Adam自适应矩更新；4) 采用四阶段矩预热课程，逐步扩展训练时间窗口，将全局非凸问题转化为局部良态问题序列。

Result: 1) 无噪声精确梯度实验：MSE 1.61e-14，哈密顿系数恢复至六位小数；2) 门学习实验：CNOT、iSWAP、Haar随机SU(4)均达到过程保真度1.000000；3) 量子部署实验（Qiskit Aer退极化噪声p1=0.001, p2=0.01, Nshots=1024）：三项Ising哈密顿项误差低于8%。

Conclusion: 该方法在无噪声和噪声环境下均能高精度地学习未知量子酉算子和识别哈密顿量，不依赖Trotter化或哈密顿量先验结构，具有广泛的适用性。SPSA-Adam和矩预热课程有效解决了硬件高效训练中的非凸优化难题。

Abstract: We present a variational algorithm for learning an unknown quantum unitary from time-series observable measurements, with no structural assumption about the target. The core separation: a hardware-efficient parametrised circuit learns the evolution operator U via observable matching; Hamiltonian identification follows as classical post-processing via matrix logarithm, when the target happens to be exp(-iH*tau).
  Three experiments establish the method's generality. First, a noiseless proof of correctness with exact gradients (L-BFGS-B) achieves MSE 1.61e-14 and recovers all Hamiltonian coefficients to six decimal places. Second, a gate-learning experiment fits CNOT, iSWAP, and a Haar-random SU(4) element -- none generated by any fixed Hamiltonian -- all to process fidelity 1.000000, confirming the method does not rely on Trotterisation structure. Third, quantum deployment via SPSA-Adam under Qiskit Aer depolarising noise (p1=0.001, p2=0.01, Nshots=1024) recovers all three Ising Hamiltonian terms with errors below 8%.
  The optimiser, SPSA-Adam, combines SPSA's hardware-efficient two-point gradient estimation with Adam's adaptive moment updates. A four-stage moment-warm curriculum progressively extends the training horizon, converting a global non-convex problem into a sequence of well-posed local ones.

</details>


### [76] [Collective Enhancement of Nuclear Excitation for a Nuclear Quantum Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15319)
*Pravin Kumar Dahal,Kieran Hymas,Jack Muir,James Q. Quach*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于硬X射线波导中$^{57}$Fe核集体激发的核量子电池方案，通过波形工程抑制辐射衰变并增强集体吸收，实现了超线性充电，最大能量密度约为$n\sqrt{n}$，为高能密度量子充电提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子电池受限于电子或分子激发带来的低能量密度和短保留时间，亟需探索新的物理体系以实现高能量密度的量子储能。

Method: 采用格林函数波导QED描述，提出自洽波形工程协议抑制局域辐射衰变，促进高阶集体核激发布居，在超越线性响应的饱和与驱动反作用条件下研究充电过程。

Result: 核集体激发截面增强，实现超线性充电；最大能量密度近似按$n\sqrt{n}$标度。

Conclusion: 非线性集体增强吸收是核量子电池运行的关键机制，利用现有X射线源和波导结构即可实现硬X射线波段的高能量密度量子充电。

Abstract: Current implementations of quantum batteries are constrained by limited energy density and short retention times associated with the electronic or molecular excitations. Here we propose a nuclear quantum battery based on collective excitation of the $^{57}$Fe nuclei of density $n$ embedded in a planar hard X-ray waveguide. Using a Green function waveguide-QED description, we study charging via excitation beyond linear response, where saturation and drive back-action reshape the incident pulse. We introduce a self-consistent waveform-engineering protocol that inhibits local radiative decay in the waveguide thus promoting absorption into high-lying collective nuclear excitation manifolds. We show an enhanced excitation cross section of the nuclear ensemble which yields superlinear charging, with maximum studied energy density scaling approximately like $n \sqrt{n}$. Our results provide a route to high-energy-density quantum charging at hard X-ray energies using contemporary X-ray sources and waveguide architectures by identifying nonlinear, collectively enhanced absorption as a key mechanism for nuclear quantum battery operation.

</details>


### [77] [Quantum error-correcting codes from aperiodic monotiles: the Hat and the Spectre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15326)
*Josep Batle,Adam Bednorz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Penrose平铺定义的量子纠错码扩展到非周期单调平铺（Hat和Spectre），证明了它们构成具有两个局部不可区分性类别的纠错码，其中Hat在SE(2)规范下可存储一个经典手性比特，并给出了精确的Perron-Frobenius数据。


<details>

Abstract: Non-Hermitian systems offer new opportunities for quantum optimization and computation. Here, we show that non-Hermitian quantum adiabatic algorithms require not only a real, gapped spectrum, but also a stable pseudospectrum. Abstract: In Hamiltonian systems, the only basis-independent quantity is the spectrum. Causal order, however, remains fixed by the Hamiltonian time evolution. In contrast, higher-order quantum theory permits more general processes that need not possess a definite global causal order. In the process-matrix framework, specifying a process requires a choice of subsystems corresponding to the input and output Hilbert spaces of each agent. A change of basis redefines both the agents and the corresponding decompositions into subsystems, while leaving the spectrum of the process matrix invariant. Second, we show that, in the thermodynamic limit, generic quantum processes admit a preferred decomposition with a definite causal order. Abstract: Thermodynamics imposes fundamental constraints on the evolution of quantum systems. These constraints and their dynamical consequences have been formulated within distinct paradigms, including axiomatic approaches based on quantum master equations, microscopic descriptions of open-system dynamics, and operational formulations rooted in resource theories. While each perspective has yielded important insights into thermodynamically consistent quantum dynamics, their precise relationship has remained unresolved. Here we establish the exact equivalence of these three paradigms in the Markovian regime. We prove that thermal Lindbladians satisfying Markovianity, time-translation symmetry, and quantum detailed balance are precisely those admitting a microscopic realisation as an energy-conserving thermal collision model and, equivalently, those generating Markovian thermal operations. We further provide an explicit microscopic protocol for simulating thermal Markovian processes with controlled finite-time simulation errors. We illustrate its applicability by providing faithful thermal collision-model implementations of a qubit thermalising in a bosonic environment and of a three-level autonomous thermal machine. In the latter case, the protocol gives rise to a finite-stroke thermal engine that not only reproduces the continuous-time dynamics but also its steady-state thermodynamic performance. As a whole, these results establish a unified foundation for Markovian quantum thermodynamics, showing that its axiomatic, microscopic, and operational formulations are exactly equivalent and providing a universal protocol for implementing thermal processes and machines.

Abstract: Preserving quantum resources in dissipative environments is a fundamental challenge in quantum information processing. Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical workflows are expected to underpin practical quantum computing applications, yet the quantum and HPC communities lack a shared framework for reasoning about where and when their integration requirements matter most. Such a framework must separate two distinct levels of analysis: the application level, where communication overhead affects runtime performance, and the real-time level, where it determines feasibility. To address this, we introduce a runtime model that decomposes workflow execution into quantum compute, classical compute, and communication costs. At the application level, a communication-to-computation ratio from this decomposition quantifies whether a workflow is communication-bound or compute-bound; at the real-time level, a feasibility constraint determines whether timing requirements can be met at all, with the reaction time setting the logical clock speed of fault-tolerant computation once they are. Application of this model to representative workflows demonstrates that co-location of quantum processors with HPC infrastructure offers negligible performance benefit for compute-intensive applications today, while tight integration remains crucial for real-time tasks such as quantum error correction needed for large scale quantum computations. However, we discuss how even at the application level these assessments may shift with hardware evolution, illustrating how the model can identify specific crossover conditions, and how, under fault tolerance, the reaction time can set application-level performance.

Conclusion: 在商用的CMOS兼容平台上实现了室温下单片集成量子光的产生与探测，为规模化量子光子技术提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The ability to generate and detect quantum states of light on a single integrated photonic device is essential to scale quantum photonics into useful quantum technologies. Integrating the required capabilities into complementary-metal-oxide-semiconductor compatible monolithic chips can reduce cost and unlock new functionality through miniaturisation. In this work we demonstrate a single silicon-on-insulator photonic integrated circuit for the monolithic generation and detection of quantum light on a commercially available platform that operates entirely at room temperature. Specifically, we leverage spontaneous four-wave mixing in silicon waveguides to produce squeezed light which is subsequently detected by photodiodes operating in a pulsed homodyne detector configuration on the same chip as the source. We directly measure $0.25(1)$ dB of squeezing, including contributions from waveguide propagation loss and detection inefficiency, and include a detailed analysis of the impact of nonlinear loss on the squeezing levels achievable using this platform.

Abstract: As practical quantum networks approach large-scale deployment, the need for efficient user-to-user frequency allocation is increasing, yet current approaches only provide partial solutions to the routing and spectrum allocation problem for an arbitrary quantum network. We address this challenge for repeater-less flex-grid quantum networks based on hyperentangled photons using an efficient three-stage pipeline combining leading tools in classical networking with recent advances in numerical optimization. First, double instantiations of Yen's algorithm obtain low-loss route candidates between each pair of users and the entanglement sources. Second, the advanced process optimizer (APOPT) obtains frequency channel allocations that maximize distribution rates under fidelity constraints. Finally, the constraint programming solver using satisfiability methods (CP-SAT) assigns specific frequency bins to each link, ensuring that there is no contention between frequencies from different sources. We numerically demonstrate this approach on a representative ring network and a Manhattan incumbent local exchange carrier topology, realizing significant improvements over prior genetic algorithm approaches in speed, accuracy, and scalability. Overall, this pipeline provides an efficient heuristic workflow for optimizing broadband entanglement distribution, applicable to arbitrarily connected quantum networks integrated within the existing lightwave infrastructure.

</details>

Result: Pauli算符阴影和Clifford算符阴影分别实现了局域OTOC和两点关联器的同时高效估计，贝尔采样实现了所有对角OTOC的同时计算。信息论下界完整刻画了多种情况下学习OTOC的查询复杂度，并证明了在某些场景下矢量化方法具有指数级的测量效率优势。

Conclusion: 论文成功将经典阴影方法从静态观测量扩展到了动力学关联器，建立了学习OTOC的查询复杂度下界，形式化了矢量化方法在测量效率上的优势，为量子多体动力学的有效读出提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum platforms can realize many-body dynamics beyond classical simulation yet complete readout remains intractable: the cost of extracting accessible information scales exponentially with system size. Classical shadows and Bell sampling offer scalable, multi-observable estimation from randomized or entanglement-assisted measurements. Here we aim to push these ideas beyond static snapshots to dynamical correlators, including out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) and two-point functions. In particular, we introduce the notion of the shadow of an operator, defined as the classical shadow of the vectorized time-evolved operator. Pauli operator-shadows enable simultaneous estimation of all local OTOCs, while Clifford operator-shadows enable efficient simultaneous estimation of all two-point correlators. Alternatively, Bell sampling allows one to simultaneously compute all diagonal OTOCs. We also prove information-theoretic lower bounds for learning OTOCs, fully characterizing their query complexities in many cases, and yielding exponential separations that formalize when the vectorized approach provides measurement-efficiency advantages.

</details>


### [89] [Microscopic Side Information Controls Ordered Hayden--Preskill Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15537)
*Joao V. R. Alencar,Allan R. P. Moreira,Joao B. R. Silva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Hayden-Preskill协议中输出量子比特标签不可用时的解码问题，证明了在保序删除信道下，纠缠保真度在ℓ=o(n^{2/3})时收敛到无输出值4^{-k}，在ℓ=ω(n^{2/3})且ℓ=o(n)时收敛到1，给出了恢复尺度ℓ_rec=Θ(n^{2/3})。当引入分块位置信息时，恢复尺度变为ℓ_rec(n,B)≍n^{2/3}B^{-1/3}（B≤√n）或n/B（B≥√n）。指数2/3源于随机子序列间的秩对齐巧合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hayden-Preskill协议假设解码器知道输出量子比特的微观身份标签，但实际场景中这些标签可能不可用。本文旨在探究当标签缺失、仅保留接收量子比特相对顺序时，量子信息恢复性能如何变化，并进一步分析部分位置信息对恢复能力的影响。

Method: 定义了保序删除信道，将n量子比特置乱寄存器映射到长度为ℓ的子序列。通过分析纠缠保真度在不同ℓ尺度下的收敛行为，结合随机子序列秩对齐的统计特性，推导恢复尺度。对部分位置信息场景，将寄存器分为B个连续块并揭示接收量子比特的块来源，分析恢复尺度与B的关系。

Result: 对于固定大小的日记k，最优纠缠保真度在ℓ=o(n^{2/3})时收敛到4^{-k}（与无输出相同），在ℓ=ω(n^{2/3})且ℓ=o(n)时收敛到1（对Haar随机置乱酉变换）。恢复尺度ℓ_rec=Θ(n^{2/3})。引入分块信息后，ℓ_rec(n,B)≍n^{2/3}B^{-1/3}（B≤√n）或n/B（B≥√n）。

Conclusion: 指数2/3由随机子序列间的秩对齐巧合控制，这同时影响了可达性证明和逆论证。即使仅获得接收子系统来源的经典信息，也能改变Hayden-Preskill恢复所需的量子输出量。

Abstract: In the Hayden--Preskill protocol, the decoder is usually assumed to know the microscopic identity of the collected output qubits. We study what happens when these labels are unavailable and only the relative order of the received qubits is preserved. The resulting order-preserving deletion channel maps an $n$-qubit scrambled register to a subsequence of length $\ell$. For a diary of fixed size $k$, we prove that the optimal entanglement fidelity converges to the no-output value $4^{-k}$ when $\ell=o(n^{2/3})$, uniformly over the scrambling unitary. For a Haar-random scrambling unitary, it converges to one when $\ell=ω(n^{2/3})$ and $\ell=o(n)$. Monotonicity then gives the fixed-error recovery scale $\ell_{\mathrm{rec}}=Θ(n^{2/3})$. We also consider partial position information obtained by dividing the register into $B$ consecutive blocks and revealing the block of origin of each received qubit. The recovery scale becomes $\ell_{\mathrm{rec}}(n,B)\asymp n^{2/3}B^{-1/3}$ for $B\leq\sqrt n$ and $\ell_{\mathrm{rec}}(n,B)\asymp n/B$ for $B\geq\sqrt n$. The exponent $2/3$ is traced to rank-aligned coincidences between random subsequences, which control both the converse and the recovery argument. Thus, even purely classical information about the origin of the received subsystems can change the amount of quantum output required for Hayden--Preskill recovery.

</details>


### [90] [Benchmarking Hybrid Quantum-Classical Algorithms for Power Grid Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15543)
*Igor Gaidai,Rick Mukherjee*

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了混合量子-经典变分算法在求解交流最优潮流机组组合（AC-OPF-UC）问题中的应用，提出了两种候选算法：一种是直接对问题完全离散化并应用QAOA，另一种是仅在量子计算机上编码二进制机组状态变量而连续变量经典求解。实验表明，在5到13个发电机的测试实例中，高效量子比特的混合方法并未优于均匀随机采样，且直接离散化方法所需的量子比特数量过大。作者认为需要测试更大规模系统（25+发电机）或探索其他方法（如量子分支定界）才能确立潜在优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索混合量子-经典变分算法能否在解决单周期AC-OPF-UC问题上相对于经典方法带来改进。AC-OPF-UC是一个困难的混合整数非线性优化问题，传统方法计算困难，而量子计算可能提供新的解决途径。

Method: 提出两种混合量子-经典方法：1) 将问题完全离散化后应用QAOA，通过惩罚项和松弛变量处理约束；2) 仅将二进制机组状态变量编码到量子计算机，连续功率流变量在经典计算机上针对每个采样比特串进行优化。在5-13个发电机的随机实例上与SCIP、SMAC和均匀随机采样进行对比。

Result: 直接离散化方法所需量子比特数过大，实用性差；高效量子比特的混合方法在5-13个发电机实例上未能超越均匀随机采样，表明当前方法在测试规模下无优势。

Conclusion: 需要测试更大规模系统（25+发电机）才能判断该混合变分策略是否具有潜在优势，但当前计算能力无法支持；或者需要探索其他方法，如量子版本的分支定界算法。

Abstract: Alternating Current Optimal Power Flow Unit Commitment (AC-OPF-UC) is a difficult mixed-integer nonlinear optimization problem that combines binary generator commitment decisions with nonconvex continuous AC power-flow constraints. In this work, we investigate whether hybrid quantum-classical variational algorithms can improve the solution of single-period AC-OPF-UC relative to classical approaches. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to directly evaluate quantum or hybrid quantum-classical algorithms for the full AC-OPF-UC problem. We consider two candidate algorithms for improving AC-OPF-UC solution quality relative to purely classical methods on ideal quantum hardware. The first applies QAOA directly to a fully discretized formulation of the problem, with equality and inequality constraints incorporated through penalty terms and slack variables. Although conceptually straightforward, this approach requires a prohibitively large number of qubits even for small instances. The second, qubit-efficient approach encodes only the binary generator status variables on a quantum computer, while optimizing the continuous power-flow variables classically for each sampled bitstring. We benchmark this method on randomly generated AC-OPF-UC instances with 5 to 13 generators and compare it against SCIP, SMAC, and uniform random sampling. Our simulations show that the qubit-efficient hybrid method does not outperform uniform sampling. These results suggest that in order to establish potential advantage of the variational hybrid strategy considered here over the best classical algorithms, if any, much larger system sizes (25+ generators) need to be tested, which is beyond our computational capacity. Alternatively, different approaches, such as quantum versions of branch-and-bound methods, may be more promising.

</details>


### [91] [Quantum states supported by matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15548)
*Xiaowei Huang,Fei Shi,Lijun Zhang,Lvzhou Li*

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子态与拟阵理论之间的结构对应关系，证明了量子态的关键性质（如纠缠和测量）可以通过拟阵在纯组合意义上刻画。核心结论包括：拟阵支撑态在拟阵连通时是真正纠缠的；拟阵所有基的均匀叠加态真正纠缠当且仅当拟阵连通；Z基局域测量后得到的新态，其底层拟阵是原拟阵的拟阵子式；受拟阵对偶启发，提出了量子态对偶的概念，揭示了态变换中的深层结构对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合量子信息理论与组合数学（拟阵理论）之间的概念鸿沟，通过纯组合工具来刻画量子态的纠缠结构和测量变换，从而为量子态的复杂性提供新的数学视角。

Method: 建立拟阵支撑态（matroid-supported state）的概念，将量子态与拟阵的基（bases）对应起来；利用拟阵的连通性（connectedness）刻画纠缠性质；通过拟阵子式（minor）描述测量后的态结构；引入拟阵对偶（matroid duality）类比量子态对偶。

Result: (1) 拟阵支撑态真正纠缠当且仅当其底层拟阵是连通的；(2) 拟阵所有基的均匀叠加态真正纠缠当且仅当拟阵连通；(3) Z基局域测量后，新态的底层拟阵是原拟阵的子式；(4) 提出量子态对偶概念，揭示结构对称性。

Conclusion: 拟阵理论为量子态的纠缠分类和测量变换提供了纯组合的、结构化的描述框架，量子态对偶揭示了此前未被注意的对称性，有望推动量子信息与组合数学的交叉研究。

Abstract: In this work, we establish a structural correspondence between quantum states and matroid theory. This connection demonstrates that key properties of quantum states, including entanglement and measurement, can be characterized in purely combinatorial terms via matroids, despite the apparent conceptual distance between these two fields. Using this framework, we show that a matroid-supported state is genuinely entangled when its underlying matroid is connected. Moreover, a uniform superposition over all bases of a matroid is genuinely entangled if and only if the matroid is connected. We also demonstrate that a local measurement in the $Z$-basis on such a state yields another matroid-supported state, whose underlying matroid is a minor of the original one. Inspired by matroid duality, we further propose a notion of quantum state duality, uncovering a deep structural symmetry in state transformations.

</details>


### [92] [Agnostic learning of qudit stabilizer states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15559)
*Wentao Qi,Boyan Xu,Shiguang Feng,Lvzhou Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态经典描述的学习是量子计算的基本任务。尽管qubit系统中稳定子态的agnostic学习已被Chen等人解决，但qudit系统因结构差异无法直接推广，该问题尚未被探索。

Method: 将稳定子自举框架推广到qudit系统，利用单份和四份拷贝测量，设计算法输出与未知态保真度至少为$τ- \varepsilon$的稳定子态。

Result: 首个针对qudit稳定子态agnostic学习的高效量子算法，复杂度为$(d/τ)^{O(d^2 \log(1/τ))} \cdot \mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$；同时给出高保真度场景的简化算法。

Conclusion: 该算法可直接用于高效估计量子态的magic（以稳定子保真度量化），完善了qudit稳定子态agnostic学习的理论图景。

Abstract: Learning a classical description of a quantum state is a fundamental task in quantum computation. Among the most important classes of quantum states are stabilizer states, which play a central role in quantum error correction and fault-tolerant computation. To mitigate the effects of realistic noise, agnostic learning of stabilizer states has emerged as a natural and well-motivated problem. Recently, Chen \textit{et al.} [STOC'25, p. 429-438] resolved this problem for qubit systems by using a stabilizer bootstrapping framework. However, the agnostic learning of qudit stabilizer states remains largely unexplored, since the qudit setting introduces fundamental structural differences that prevent a direct generalization of existing qubit techniques. In this paper, we successfully generalize the stabilizer bootstrapping framework to qudit systems and present the first efficient quantum algorithm for agnostic learning of qudit stabilizer states. Specifically, given copies of an unknown $n$-qudit pure state $|ψ\rangle$ that has fidelity $τ$ with some stabilizer state, our algorithm outputs a stabilizer state $|φ\rangle$ such that $\left| \braket{φ|ψ} \right|^2 \geq τ- \varepsilon$ with high probability. The algorithm uses only single-copy and four-copy measurements, and its sample and time complexity scale as $(d/τ)^{O(d^2 \log(1/τ))} \cdot \mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$, where the dimension $d$ is an odd prime. As a direct corollary, our algorithm enables efficient estimation of the magic of a quantum state, as quantified by its stabilizer fidelity. Completing the picture, we also present a streamlined algorithm for the high-fidelity regime $τ> \cos^2(π/8)$, establishing a qudit analogue of the threshold-based approach in prior qubit work.

</details>
