*Mu Qiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于里德伯原子和 trapped ion 之间 C4 力（电荷诱导偶极力）的快速可控 Z 门，结合自旋依赖的光学 Magnus 力，在微秒级闭合相空间轨迹，实现混合原子-离子量子计算架构，支持混合 qLDPC 存储器和短距离 QCCD 链路，电路级蒙特卡罗模拟表明该架构在固定码距和逻辑错误率下比纯原子或纯离子架构支持多个数量级的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列和 trapped ion 晶体在容错量子计算中各有优势，但缺乏一种快速确定性的相互作用方式，亟需一种能够高效耦合两类平台的方案以发挥互补优势。

Method: 利用里德伯激发原子与 trapped ion 之间的 C4 力（电荷诱导偶极力）产生相互作用，同时通过自旋依赖的光学 Magnus 力对离子施加平衡作用，使得相空间轨迹在几微秒内闭合，实现可控 Z 门；通过切换里德伯态将方案扩展到多离子晶体，实现混合 qLDPC 存储器架构。

Result: 混合架构支持约 5 kHz 的原子穿梭速度，加速短距离 QCCD 链路；电路级蒙特卡罗模拟表明，在固定码距和逻辑错误率下，混合架构比纯原子或纯离子架构支持多个数量级的操作。

Conclusion: 提出的混合原子-离子量子计算架构能够有效结合两类平台的互补优势，显著提升容错量子计算的可扩展性和操作能力，为实现大规模容错量子计算提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Neutral-atom arrays and trapped-ion crystals offer complementary strengths for fault-tolerant quantum computing but lack a fast way to deterministically interact. Here we propose a controlled-$Z$ gate generated by the charge-induced-dipole ($C_4$) force between a Rydberg-excited atom and a trapped ion, balanced by a spin-dependent optical Magnus force on the ion that closes phase-space trajectories within a few microseconds. Toggling the Rydberg state extends the scheme to multi-ion crystals at negligible overhead. The resulting ${\sim}5\,$kHz atom shuttle accelerates short-distance QCCD links and enables hybrid qLDPC memories in which atom logical qubits are written onto an ion block treated as a passive storage zone. We perform circuit-level Monte Carlo simulations and find that the hybrid architecture supports orders of magnitude more operations than atom-only or ion-only architectures at fixed code distance and logical error rate.

</details>


### [94] [Classical codes violate the conjectured square-root bound for quantum random access codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15617)
*Kangqiao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，具有密度算子编码和任意解码测量的量子随机存取码（QRAC）能违反此前猜想的$p\leq(1+\sqrt{m/n})/2$界限。通过将经典随机存取码嵌入为QRAC（使用对角编码态和对易解码测量），作者构造了纯态实现，并在固定压缩率下，对任意$p\in(1/2,1)$和足够大的输入长度实现了违反。有限块长分析进一步给出了阶最优的对数量子比特标度。结果揭示经典编码率是分离的根源，并激励基于解码测量谱性质的受限界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验量子随机存取码（QRAC）是否普遍满足猜想的界限$p\leq(1+\sqrt{m/n})/2$，其中$n$比特经典信息编码为$m$个量子比特，$p$为最坏情况成功概率。

Method: 1. 发现带有私有随机性的经典随机存取码（QRAC的子类）能违反猜想界；2. 将这些经典码嵌入为QRAC，使用对角化编码态和对易解码测量；3. 构造具有相同解码统计量的纯态实现；4. 利用Ambainis等人的可达性定理构造违反实例；5. 进行有限块长分析。

Result: 1. 对每个固定压缩率，在$(1/2,1)$区间内任意$p$均存在违反猜想界的QRAC；2. 有限块长下，恢复偏差标度为$\sqrt{\log_2 n/n}$时，量子比特数呈对数增长（阶最优）。

Conclusion: 经典编码率是导致猜想界与Nayak界之间分离的根本原因；需要基于解码测量定量谱性质提出新的受限界。

Abstract: We consider whether every quantum random access code (QRAC) with density-operator encodings and arbitrary decoding measurements obeys the conjectured bound $p\leq(1+\sqrt{m/n})/2$, where $n$ classical bits are encoded into $m$ qubits and $p$ is the worst-case success probability. We find that classical random access codes with private randomness, which form a subclass of this QRAC model, violate the bound. We embed these classical codes as QRACs with diagonal encoding states and commuting decoding measurements, and construct pure-state realizations with identical decoding statistics. The achievability theorem of Ambainis, Nayak, Ta-Shma, and Vazirani then yields violations for every fixed $p\in(1/2,1)$ at sufficiently large input length. The counterexamples span the full open interval between the conjectured and Nayak bounds at each fixed compression rate. A finite-blocklength analysis further yields order-optimal logarithmic qubit scaling for a recovery bias scaling as $\sqrt{\log_2 n/n}$ with a sufficiently large prefactor. These results identify the classical coding rate as the source of the separation and motivate restricted bounds based on quantitative spectral properties of decoding measurements.

</details>


### [95] [Fate of dynamical quantum phase transitions from sudden quench to slow quench limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15649)
*Xiang Zhang,Fuxiang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了从突然淬火到慢淬火过程中动态量子相变（DQPT）的行为，发现慢淬火能滤除偶然DQPT，保留与平衡量子相变本质相关的DQPT，从而使其成为探测平衡量子临界点的动力学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对DQPT的研究主要集中在突然淬火协议上，缺少对慢淬火区域系统性的探索，尤其是DQPT在慢淬火下的鲁棒性及其与平衡量子相变的内在联系尚不清楚。

Method: 作者系统比较了从突然淬火到慢淬火的不同协议，分析了DQPT的演化行为，并利用XY链、Aubry-André模型和三聚体Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型进行理论验证。

Result: 慢淬火协议下，跨越平衡量子相变时DQPT保持鲁棒；而前后哈密顿量处于同一平衡相的偶然DQPT则被滤除。慢淬火揭示了DQPT与平衡量子相变之间的本质联系。

Conclusion: 慢淬火能够区分本质性与偶然性DQPT，从而使DQPT成为识别平衡量子临界点的可靠动力学探针。

Abstract: Previous investigations of dynamical quantum phase transition (DQPT) have predominantly focused on sudden quench protocols. In this work, we systematically explore the fate of DQPT from the sudden to the slow quench regime. We establish that DQPT remains robust under slow quenching when the protocol crosses an equilibrium quantum phase transition. Conversely, we show that accidental DQPTs,which occur when the pre and post quench Hamiltonians reside in the same equilibrium phase, can be filtered out in the slow quench limit. This demonstrates that slow quenches can reveal the intrinsic connection between DQPT and equilibrium quantum phase transitions, thereby enabling DQPT to serve as a dynamical probe for identifying equilibrium quantum critical points. We illustrate these findings with examples from the XY chain, the Aubry-André model, and the trimer Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model.

</details>


### [96] [Spectral Attack on Continuous-Variable Quantum Key Distribution Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15668)
*Chen Gong,Mingxuan Guo,Peng Huang,Tao Wang,Guihua Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了连续变量量子密钥分发（CVQKD）系统中由发射端与接收端带宽不匹配导致的实际安全漏洞，并提出一种新的频谱攻击方案。与传统方法不同，该方案无需引入额外噪声即可直接窃取原始密钥信息。实验验证了攻击的可行性，并提出了相应的防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVQKD系统中发射端与接收端之间存在不同程度的带宽不匹配，这可能导致携带调制信息的频率分量无法被合法方完全感知，从而产生实际安全漏洞。现有研究尚未充分认识并利用这一漏洞进行攻击，因此需要识别该漏洞并评估其威胁。

Method: 提出了一种频谱攻击方案，利用带宽不匹配漏洞直接获取原始密钥信息，无需像传统截获重发攻击那样引入额外噪声来掩盖操作。通过构建基于滤波操作的CVQKD系统进行原理验证实验。

Result: 实验证明，若忽略该实际安全漏洞，窃听者Eve能够获取足够的信息使系统变得不安全。该攻击方案能够在不引入额外扰动的情况下直接获取原始密钥信息。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于弥合理论模型与实际实现之间的差距，为实际量子通信系统中的防御设计提供了参考。基于识别的安全漏洞，提出了相应的防御策略。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CVQKD) has attracted extensive attention due to its compatibility and low costs. However, bandwidth mismatch exists to varying degrees between the transmitter and receiver. This may prevent frequency components carrying modulation information from being fully perceived by the legitimate party. In this paper, we identify a practical security loophole caused by bandwidth mismatch and propose a corresponding spectral attack scheme. Different from previous approaches that exploit security loopholes to conceal the excess noise introduced by intercept-resend attacks, this scheme can directly obtain raw-key information without introducing additional disturbances. A proof-of-principle attack on a CVQKD system with filtering operation is constructed to verify the feasibility. Experimental results indicate that Eve can obtain enough information to render the system insecure if this practical security loophole is ignored. Based on the identified security loophole, corresponding defense strategies are proposed. This work helps bridge the gap between theoretical models and practical implementations, providing a reference for defense design in practical quantum communication systems.

</details>


### [97] [Boundary-Phase Control of Sequentially Addressed Trapped-Ion ZZ Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15688)
*Chun-Yang Luan,Haiyu Ding,Cheng-Kang Pan,Xiangjie Li,Lin Cheng,Gangxi Wang,Yuting Lei,Peilin Zheng,Shixin Hu,Xiang Zhang,Fei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在捕获离子量子计算中，通过交替寻址通道施加非重叠状态依赖力来实现双离子门的方法。通过复振幅近共振描述，将每个时间窗口产生的位移矢量进行辛乘积以产生ZZ相位，并设计了匹配包络的相位和对比度控制方案。在代表性参数下，条件拉姆齐协议达到0.998/0.996的对比度，尽管顺序控制在归一化平方力作用上比同步控制高1.826倍，但为实验提供了可测试的控制资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动介导的捕获离子门通常对两个目标离子同步施加状态依赖力。本文考虑了一种互补设置，其中一个寻址通道在两个目标之间交替切换，使得力的作用窗口不重叠。这种交替寻址方案在实验上具有吸引力，但需要建立相应的理论框架和控制方法。

Method: 采用复振幅近共振描述，每个窗口生成位移矢量，不同离子位移矢量的有序辛乘积产生ZZ相位。设计了匹配包络的相位和对比度控制方案，区分边界相位响应与闭合误差及暗间隙中的残余力。波形合成抑制微分运动，而约化自旋映射（在线性自旋依赖力模型内精确）处理投影公共运动、确定性局域Z相位、旁观者耦合和控制参数漂移。

Result: 条件拉姆齐协议预测连续和重置对比度分别为0.998和0.996。重置规则将提取的配对相位相对于连续规则重定向0.581 rad（模π/2）。顺序控制每次仅使用一个主动目标通道，但所需归一化平方力作用是独立校准同步控制的1.826倍。

Conclusion: 在代表性实验参数和所述近似范围内，窗口间力正交参考为顺序捕获离子相互作用提供了一种实验可测试的控制资源。交替寻址方案虽然消耗更多力作用，但为实验实现提供了灵活性。

Abstract: Motion-mediated trapped-ion gates typically apply synchronized state-dependent forces to both target ions. We consider the complementary setting in which one addressing channel alternates between the targets, so their force windows do not overlap. In a complex-amplitude near-resonant description, each window generates a displacement vector, and ordered symplectic products of vectors on different ions produce the ZZ phase. For fixed envelopes and timings, relative rotations of the window displacement vectors redirect the sign and magnitude of this phase, whereas a common rotation of all displacement vectors leaves the pair phase invariant. Motivated by experimental alternating-addressing protocols, we design matched-envelope phase and contrast controls that distinguish the boundary-phase response from closure error and residual force in dark gaps. Waveform synthesis suppresses differential motion, while a reduced-spin map that is exact within the linear spin-dependent-force model accounts for projector-common motion, deterministic local-Z phases, spectator coupling, and control-parameter drift. A conditional-Ramsey protocol predicts continuous and reset contrasts of 0.998 and 0.996, with the reset rule redirecting the extracted pair phase by 0.581 rad relative to the continuous rule, modulo pi/2. In the representative comparison, sequential control uses only one active target channel at a time, but requires 1.826 times the normalized squared-force action of independently calibrated simultaneous control. For representative experimental parameters and within the stated approximations, the inter-window force-quadrature reference therefore provides an experimentally testable control resource for sequential trapped-ion interactions.

</details>


### [98] [Non-Gaussianity in cat codes: global incompatibility and local geometric alignment with the magic resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15739)
*Yuwei Zhu,Seungbeom Chin,William J. Munro,Kae Nemoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了猫码（cat-code）框架下非高斯性（NG）与魔法资源（magic resource）之间的对应关系，发现与GKP编码不同，猫码不存在全局值保持的等价性，但通过渐近Wigner对数负性（WLN）几何与魔法度量几何之间可建立局部对齐关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GKP编码框架中，非高斯性与魔法资源之间存在精确对应关系，但该关系是否在GKP编码之外（如猫码）依然成立尚不清楚。本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 通过比较Wigner对数负性（WLN）与基于相位算子基定义的魔法度量，分析了非简并d-峰猫态的资源几何；在SU(d)渐近猫码下，基于固有局部几何建立了WLN水平集与魔法度量水平集之间的扇区依赖对齐。

Result: 猫码不继承GKP那种全局值保持的魔法-非高斯等价性，但其渐近WLN几何允许与魔法度量几何构建局部对齐关系。

Conclusion: 猫码中魔法与非高斯资源之间存在全局不兼容但局部可构建的关系，提示了GKP框架之外基于几何的资源对应研究方法。

Abstract: Non-Gaussianity is an essential resource for genuine quantum advantages in continuous-variable quantum systems and is regarded as a counterpart of the magic resource in discrete-variable systems. Recently, an exact correspondence between non-Gaussianity (NG) and the magic resource was identified within the Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill (GKP) encoding framework. Whether such a relation persists beyond GKP encoding, however, remains unclear. Here, we address this question in the cat-code setting. By comparing the Wigner logarithmic negativity (WLN) and a magic measure defined from a phase-operator basis, we analyze the resource geometry of non-degenerate $d$-peaked cat states. We find that cat codes do not inherit the global value-preserving GKP magic--NG equivalence, but their asymptotic WLN geometry allows a constructive local alignment with the magic-measure geometry. Specifically, under a distinguished SU($d$) asymptotic cat code, we establish a sector-dependent alignment between WLN level sets and magic-measure level sets based on their intrinsic local geometries. These results identify both the global incompatibility and the locally constructive relation between magic and non-Gaussian resources in cat codes, suggesting a geometry-based approach to resource correspondences beyond the GKP framework.

</details>


### [99] [Grover's algorithm for image edge detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15744)
*Pulak Ranjan Giri,Kazuhiro Saito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Grover算法加速数字图像边缘检测，相比经典算法实现二次加速，且在噪声环境下具有高成功概率，已在两量子比特Qiskit电路上实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子边缘检测方法在噪声环境下成功率较低，而Grover算法本身具有高成功概率的特性，适合当前NISQ设备上的实际应用。

Method: 利用Grover算法搜索标记元素的速度优势，将其应用于数字图像边缘检测；对图像进行2×2分块处理，使用两量子比特Qiskit电路在QASM模拟器和IBM Sydney（fake）噪声设备上实现。

Result: 该方法在检测图像边缘时具有高成功概率，优于现有大多数量子边缘检测方法，且适用于当前NISQ设备。

Conclusion: 基于Grover算法的量子边缘检测方法在噪声环境下表现优越，是当前量子设备上进行图像边缘检测的更优候选方案。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm is one of the most significant quantum algorithms, enabling the search for a marked element in an unsorted database quadratically faster than classical search algorithms. We utilize Groves's algorithm to detect the edges of a digital image faster than any classical edge detection methods. The high success probability of our method in detecting image edges, compared to most existing quantum edge detection methods, makes it a more suitable candidate for use in the presence of noise on current quantum devices. We implemented our model for the $2\times 2$ blocks of the image using a two-qubit Qiskit circuit with the QASM simulator and the noisy IBM Sydney(fake) device, making it suitable for most of the current NISQ devices.

</details>


### [100] [Manipulating non-intrinsic outputs of a non-Hermitian coupled system by weak external driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15770)
*He Feng,Yi-Fan Xiang,Wei Li,Xue-Hua Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了非厄米量子耦合系统中耗散导致的自激发与耦合激发谱函数之间的干涉，产生非本征输出，可通过弱驱动强度比有效调控，并发现了联系本征能级分裂、光谱拉比劈裂与平均总衰减率的毕达哥拉斯关系，为精确测量本征能级分裂和耦合强度提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为测量中的弱外部驱动对被测系统的本征输出扰动可忽略不计，但本文质疑这一假设，旨在探究非厄米量子耦合系统中耗散如何影响测量输出，并揭示其背后机制。

Method: 通过分析非厄米量子耦合系统中自激发与耦合激发谱函数的干涉效应，研究弱驱动强度比对系统输出的调控作用，并推导出本征能级分裂、光谱拉比劈裂与平均总衰减率之间的毕达哥拉斯关系。

Result: 发现非本征输出可通过双通道弱驱动强度比有效调控，产生高损耗模向低损耗模收敛的极端耗散抑制效应，以及光谱拉比劈裂被巨大谐振频率偏移替代的现象。数值模拟证实经典耦合系统中也存在干涉输出。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对测量过程和非厄米物理的理解，提出的毕达哥拉斯关系为精确测量本征能级分裂和耦合强度提供了新途径。

Abstract: Measurement provides access to exploring and understanding nature and finds widespread applications. It is generally believed that the weak external driving used in measurement introduces negligible disturbance to the intrinsic output of the probed system. Here, we reveal that dissipation in a non-Hermitian quantum coupled system induces interference between self-excitation and coupling-excitation spectral functions, giving rise to non-intrinsic outputs. These non-intrinsic outputs can be effectively manipulated by the ratio of weak external driving strengths applied to two channels, leading to significant phenomena: extreme suppression of dissipation by convergence effect of high-loss mode towards low-loss mode, and giant shift of resonant frequency instead of original spectral Rabi splitting. Crucially, we discover the Pythagorean relation linking eigenlevel splitting, spectral Rabi splitting and average total decay rate, enabling precise measurement of the eigenlevel splitting and coupling strength. Numerical simulations confirm that the interference outputs also exist in classical coupled systems. Our work provides a profound insight into measurement and non-Hermitian physics.

</details>


### [101] [The Fourier Wall: Why Public Tabular Datasets Refuse Quantum Advantage, and a Certified Recipe for Where It Lives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15815)
*Javier Mancilla,Tomás Tagliani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"傅里叶墙"理论，指出角度编码量子神经网络本质上为部分傅里叶级数，只有在目标频谱同时满足离格、高阶相互作用（≥3阶）、高频、近独立特征且稠密不可穷举五个条件时，量子优势才可能显现。基于此设计了SPECTRA双层认证框架，在工业智能电表数据上验证：经典梯度提升树在真实峰值负荷任务中达0.999 ROC-AUC，而针对量子过程生成标签的任务，匹配的量子模型达0.994 vs 经典最优0.699，且当相互作用耦合被消融时性能崩塌。经典精确模拟器在小宽度下可匹配量子模型但指数级计算成本，估计硬件交叉点在13-19站点附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子机器学习在公开表格基准上通常败给精心调优的经典基线，作者认为这并非当前模型能力的局限，而是数据集的结构性属性所致。需要理解何时量子优势才可能真正出现，并为识别和验证这种优势提供可操作的标准。

Method: 提出傅里叶墙五条件理论，设计SPECTRA双层认证框架：第一层为无模拟器的结构筛选，第二层通过配对自助置信界在匹配量子模型与五个调优经典孪生模型之间进行决定性对比。

Result: 工业智能电表数据上：真实峰值负荷任务中梯度提升树达0.999 ROC-AUC；量子过程生成标签的任务中，匹配量子模型达0.994 vs 经典最优0.699，消融相互作用后性能崩塌。经典模拟器在小宽度可匹配但指数成本，估计硬件交叉点13-19站点。

Conclusion: 这些结果为在表格数据中识别、工程化和部署量子优势候选方案提供了实用方法。

Abstract: Across public tabular benchmarks, quantum machine-learning (QML) models usually lose to carefully tuned classical baselines. We argue that this is a structural property of the datasets rather than merely a limitation of current models. Because an angle-encoded quantum neural network is a partial Fourier series, a genuine advantage can arise only when the target spectrum is simultaneously off-grid, of interaction order at least three, high-frequency, supported by near-independent features, and dense beyond practical enumeration. We call the failure to satisfy these conditions the Fourier wall. We operationalize the conditions as SPECTRA, a two-tier certificate: a simulator-free structural screen followed by a decisive comparison between a matched quantum model and five tuned classical twins using paired-bootstrap confidence bounds. On industrial smart-meter data, SPECTRA correctly refuses the real peak-load target, for which gradient-boosted trees reach a held-out ROC-AUC of 0.999. On the same real energy phases with labels generated by an interacting quantum process, the dynamics-matched quantum model reaches 0.994 versus 0.699 for the best generic classical baseline, and collapses to chance when its interaction couplings are ablated. An exact classical simulator ties the quantum model at small width but incurs measured exponential evaluation cost, with an estimated hardware crossover near 13-19 sites. These results provide a practical recipe for identifying, engineering, and deploying quantum-advantage candidates in tabular data.

</details>


### [102] [Maximal quantum leakage: operational interpretation and quantum channel analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15853)
*Shuixin Xiao,Zijia Zhao,Jingge Zhu,Farhad Farokhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了最大量子泄漏与等先验最小错误量子态判别问题等价，建立了可计算的运算解释，分析了集体测量对泄漏的影响，并提出了联合设计输入态和测量的迭代算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大量子泄漏此前难以计算，本文旨在建立其可计算的操作性解释，并分析集体测量对泄漏的影响。

Method: 通过将最大量子泄漏问题等价转化为等先验的最小错误量子态判别问题，分析集体测量对多副本量子态泄漏的单调增长特性，并开发联合设计输入态和测量的迭代算法。

Result: 证明了最大量子泄漏与等先验最小错误量子态判别的等价性；展示了集体测量下泄漏随副本数单调增长并渐近表征；开发了针对量子信道的迭代设计算法。

Conclusion: 最大量子泄漏获得了可计算的运算解释，集体测量在渐近极限下的泄漏特征得以明确，所提算法为量子信道中隐私保护的输入态和测量设计提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Maximal quantum leakage quantifies privacy against adversaries with arbitrary intentions. In this work, we prove that computing this leakage is equivalent to minimum-error quantum state discrimination with equal priors. This establishes a computable operational interpretation, addressing the previous difficulty in computing maximal quantum leakage. We further analyze the impact of collective measurements on multiple copies of a state, demonstrating that leakage increases monotonically with the number of copies, which leads to explicitly characterizing the maximal leakage in the asymptotic limit. Extending this framework to quantum channels, we develop an iterative algorithm for the jointly designing of input states and measurements. Numerical examples involving collective measurements and the maximal channel leakage demonstrate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [103] [The statistical disturbance bound of quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15874)
*Ties-A. Ohst,Sebastian Schlösser,Roope Uola*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统计扰动界，通过连接量子测量的统计特性与任何兼容测量通道引起的状态扰动，无需精确知道测量通道即可量化测量扰动。并开发了加权态排除技术，可在未知测量效应的情况下实验测定该扰动界。该方法优于现有信息-扰动关系，能区分信息量等价的测量，且可在测量算子层析重建不充分的条件下检测扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化量子测量引起的扰动通常需要知晓底层测量通道的详细信息，这在实际中往往难以实现。本文旨在建立一种无需详尽测量通道知识即可量化测量扰动的统计方法。

Method: 引入了统计扰动界，将量子测量（描述为POVM）的统计特性与兼容测量通道引起的状态扰动联系起来；开发了加权态排除技术，可在无需明确知道测量效应的情况下实验确定该扰动界。

Result: 该方法能区分信息量等价的测量；可在测量算子层析重建不充分的条件下检测和量化测量诱导扰动；可应用于量子随机数生成协议中限制窃听者的猜测概率。

Conclusion: 提出的统计扰动界和加权态排除技术为量化量子测量扰动提供了更灵活实用的框架，克服了传统方法对测量通道详细知识的依赖，并在量子密码学中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Quantifying the disturbance caused by a quantum measurement typically requires detailed knowledge of the underlying measurement channel. In this work, we introduce a statistical disturbance bound, which connects the statistical properties of a quantum measurement to the state disturbance induced by any compatible measurement channel. Specifically, we show that the average fidelity between input and output with respect to an arbitrary ensemble of pure input states is fundamentally bounded in terms of the measurement, described as a positive operator-valued measure (POVM). We further develop the weighted state exclusion technique, which enables an experimental determination of the statistical disturbance bound without requiring explicit knowledge of the measurement effects. To see the advantages of our approach over existing information-disturbance relations, we show that our bound distinguishes between measurements with equivalent informativeness. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the weighted state exclusion technique can detect and quantify measurement-induced disturbance using state preparations that are insufficient for tomographic reconstruction of the measurement operators. Finally, we illustrate how disturbance bounds defined with respect to specific input ensembles can be used to bound an eavesdropper's guessing probability in a simple protocol for quantum randomness generation.

</details>


### [104] [Alleviating the Sparse Matrix Scaling Bottleneck in Adaptive VQE via Greedy Operator Commutativity Partitioning and High-Order Taylor State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15906)
*Hermawan Kresno Dipojono*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出GOCP框架，通过五阶泰勒展开链替代显式矩阵指数化，将酉演化简化为稀疏矩阵-向量运算，实现了对超过2.68×10^8个算子流形的高效模拟，在BeH₂和强相关H₂O分子系统中保持了高于1-10⁻⁶的态保真度和亚化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE及其自适应变体（如ADAPT-VQE）在强关联量子系统研究中至关重要，但算子空间的指数缩放和酉演化的高昂计算成本严重制约了经典模拟。需要一种可扩展的分析方法来解决这些瓶颈。

Method: 提出GOCP框架，将复杂酉旋转重构为五阶O(5)泰勒级数展开的链式序列，从而避免显式矩阵指数化，将计算任务简化为稀疏矩阵-向量操作的序列。

Result: 在BeH₂和强相关H₂O分子系统上，使用Jordan-Wigner和Bravyi-Kitaev两种映射均取得优异结果：态保真度超过1-10⁻⁶，基态能量达到亚化学精度，且算子流形规模可超过2.68×10⁸个元素。

Conclusion: GOCP框架通过避免显式矩阵指数化并提供高效算子选择与态演化优化，为复杂量子多体系统中深度变分电路的模拟提供了一条可扩展且严谨的途径。

Abstract: The Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and its adaptive variants, such as ADAPT-VQE, are central to the study of strongly correlated quantum systems. However, the classical simulation of the ansatz growth process remains constrained by the exponential scaling of operator space and the associated computational cost of unitary evolution. We introduce the Greedy Operator Commutativity Partitioning (GOCP) framework, an analytical methodology designed to optimize both operator selection and state evolution. By reformulating complex unitary rotations as a chained sequence of fifth-order O(5) Taylor series expansions, GOCP bypasses the need for explicit matrix exponentiation, reducing the computational task to a sequence of sparse matrix-vector operations. We evaluate the performance of this framework across diverse molecular systems, including BeH2 and strongly correlated H2O geometries, utilizing both Jordan-Wigner and Bravyi-Kitaev mappings. Our results demonstrate that the GOCP framework maintains exceptional numerical fidelity-exceeding 1 - 10^-6 in state fidelity-while achieving sub-chemical accuracy in ground-state energy calculations. By enabling the simulation of operator manifolds exceeding 2.68 x 10^8 elements with high efficiency, this approach provides a scalable and rigorous pathway for exploring deep variational circuits in complex quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [105] [Reconstruction of the noise correlation spectral density from the cavity emission in a two-qubit system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15909)
*Nadine Lenke,Guido Burkard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个腔耦合量子比特在纵向噪声影响下的动力学，发现腔辐射可表征噪声相关性，并提出了从腔辐射中提取噪声相关谱密度的方法，揭示了不同噪声类型下相关效应的标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模容错量子计算中，噪声是重大挑战。除了单量子比特噪声外，噪声相关性效应同样重要，因为关联错误会破坏量子纠错。现有方法对噪声相关性的表征和提取仍不充分。

Method: 建立两个腔耦合量子比特的动力学模型，量子比特受纵向噪声影响但非直接耦合，通过腔辐射表征噪声相关性。分别分析白噪声、准静态噪声和Ornstein-Uhlenbeck噪声三种情况，利用卷积定理从不明确噪声类型中提取噪声相关谱密度。

Result: 白噪声下噪声相关效应与腔-量子比特耦合常数的五次方成比例，而准静态噪声下与三次方成比例，表明白噪声下相关效应被强烈抑制。对于已知频谱，可通过多次噪声实现平均重构噪声相关谱密度；对于未知噪声类型，可利用卷积定理提取谱密度。

Conclusion: 腔发射可作为表征量子比特噪声相关性的有效工具，且不同噪声类型具有不同的标度行为，为容错量子计算中相关噪声的识别与抑制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A significant challenge in the field of large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computation is the influence of noise. In addition to the influence of noise on individual qubits, the smaller additional effect of noise correlations is also of high significance because correlated errors pose a challenge for quantum error correction. We describe the dynamics of two cavity-coupled qubits that are subject to correlated noise, assuming that the qubits are affected by longitudinal noise and not coupled directly. We find that the cavity emission enables the characterization of the noise correlations and describe the cases of white noise, quasi-static noise, and Ornstein-Uhlenbeck noise. For a known frequency spectrum, the reconstruction of the noise correlation spectral density from the cavity emission is possible by averaging over many different noise realizations. We demonstrate that, in the case of white noise, the noise correlation effects scale with the fifth order of the cavity-qubit coupling constant and are thus strongly suppressed compared with the case of quasi-static noise, where they scale with the third order. Furthermore, we present a method for extracting the noise correlation spectral density from the cavity emission in the case where the underlying noise type remains unidentified. This can be achieved by applying the convolution theorem.

</details>


### [106] [Optimization of multistate STIRAP by pulse shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15915)
*Julian K. Dimitrov,Nikolay V. Vitanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多态STIRAP系统的脉冲整形方法，通过使暗态附近的准平行本征能量在相互作用中心区域保持平行，显著提高了布居数转移效率。该方法适用于仅具有最近邻耦合的多态链，并推导了五态、七态和九态链的解析脉冲波形。数值模拟显示，优化脉冲可将转移误差降低数个数量级，同时增强对多种参数变化的鲁棒性，并在自发辐射存在时仍保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展准平行本征能量方法（原用于两态和三态系统）到多态链，在不引入额外捷径场的前提下改善多态STIRAP中的布居数转移效率，克服标准高斯脉冲在高维系统中非绝热跃迁导致的误差。

Method: 基于准平行本征能量方法，设计泵浦光和斯托克斯光的脉冲形状，使最接近暗态的本征能量在相互作用中心区域保持近乎平行；推导了五态、七态和九态链的解析脉冲表达式，并应用于$J_g\rightarrow J_e=J_g$和$J_g\rightarrow J_e=J_g-1$两种角动量系列的简并磁子能级系统。

Result: 数值模拟表明：优化脉冲将布居转移误差较标准高斯脉冲降低数个数量级；对峰值拉比频率、单光子失谐和多光子失谐的鲁棒性均得到改善；$J_g\rightarrow J_g$链由于Clebsch-Gordan系数使亮态能隙在暗态快速变化区域保持更大，因此表现更优；在自发辐射存在时优化脉冲仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的脉冲整形方法无需引入额外场就能有效提升多态STIRAP的转移保真度与鲁棒性，为高维量子态操控提供了实用方案。

Abstract: We propose a pulse-shaping method for improving population transfer in multistate stimulated Raman adiabatic passage (STIRAP) with nearest-neighbour couplings only. The method extends the quasiparallel-eigenenergy approach, previously used for two- and three-state systems, to multistate chains without introducing additional shortcut fields. The pump and Stokes fields are shaped so that the eigenenergies closest to the dark state remain nearly parallel to it during the central part of the interaction, where nonadiabatic transitions are most likely to occur. We derive analytic prescriptions for the required pulse shapes and apply them to five-, seven-, and nine-state chains formed by magnetic sublevels of degenerate manifolds with angular momenta $J_g\rightarrow J_e=J_g$ and $J_g\rightarrow J_e=J_g-1$ driven by a pair of right- and left-circularly polarized pulses. Numerical simulations show that the optimized pulses can reduce the population transfer error by several orders of magnitude compared to standard Gaussian pulses. The same shapes also improve robustness against variations of the peak Rabi frequency, the single-photon detuning, and the multiphoton detuning. The comparison between the two angular-momentum families shows that the $J_g\rightarrow J_g$ chains are generally more favourable, because their Clebsch-Gordan coefficients keep the relevant bright-state gap larger in the region where the dark state changes most rapidly. Finally, simulations with spontaneous emission show that the optimized resonant pulses remain advantageous in the lossy regime as well.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond the Positive Partial Transpose Squared Conjecture: The Qutrit Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15947)
*Junhyeong An,Soojoon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子纠缠交换中正偏置转置平方猜想，证明了对于qutrit完全正映射，任何1-不可蒸馏CP映射与施密特数不超过2的CP映射的复合在任何顺序下都是纠缠破坏的，并扩展到了任意选择性测量的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受PPT平方猜想的启发，该猜想询问两个PPT纠缠链路能否通过纠缠交换产生终端纠缠，等价于两个PPT映射的复合是否总是纠缠破坏的。为了研究这一问题，作者将映射复合问题推广到PPT设置之外。

Method: 通过研究qutrit完全正映射的复合性质，分析了1-不可蒸馏CP映射与施密特数不超过2的CP映射的复合行为，并考虑了任意选择性测量下的终端纠缠生成问题。

Result: 证明了任何1-不可蒸馏CP映射与施密特数不超过2的CP映射的复合在任何顺序下都是纠缠破坏的；证明了1-不可蒸馏CP映射的锥是最大的qutrit CP映射锥，使得其与每个施密特数不超过2的CP映射的复合在两个顺序下都是纠缠破坏的；证明任何1-不可蒸馏两qutrit态与任何施密特数不超过2的态在中间系统上进行任意选择性测量时都无法生成终端纠缠。

Conclusion: 本文对qutrit系统中纠缠交换和映射复合问题给出了重要结论，拓展了对PPT平方猜想及相关问题的理解。

Abstract: Entanglement swapping is a fundamental operation in quantum repeaters for establishing entanglement between distant parties. The positive partial transpose (PPT) squared conjecture asks whether two PPT entangled links can generate terminal entanglement through entanglement swapping, or equivalently, whether the composition of two PPT maps is always entanglement breaking. Motivated by this conjecture, we investigate the map-composition problem beyond the PPT setting. For qutrit completely positive (CP) maps, we prove that the composition of any CP map whose Choi matrix is $1$-undistillable with any CP map whose Choi matrix has Schmidt number at most two is entanglement breaking in either order. Moreover, we show that the cone of $1$-undistillable CP maps is exactly the largest qutrit cone of CP maps whose composition with every CP map whose Choi matrix has Schmidt number at most two is entanglement breaking in both orders. Finally, although map composition captures only the standard maximally entangled outcome in entanglement swapping, we prove that any $1$-undistillable two-qutrit state and any state of Schmidt number at most two cannot generate terminal entanglement under an arbitrary selective measurement on the intermediate systems.

</details>


### [108] [Conditional Euclidean-Hamiltonian reductions for sign-problem toy models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15950)
*Tsogtgerel Gantumur*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出条件欧几里得-哈密顿量（CEH）约化方法，将费米子体系的符号问题从标量权重重新加权转换为受控活性空间近似，通过三个基准测试验证了其在结构有限模型中的可行性，但尚未在随机框架下解决 Hubbard 模型的符号问题或证明其可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得蒙特卡洛方法在费米子有限密度体系中因路径积分权重变号或复值而失效，而哈密顿量方法则面临希尔伯特空间指数增长问题。本文旨在结合两者优势，规避复杂权重采样困难。

Method: CEH 方法将计算分为蒙特卡洛可处理的参考问题和剩余活性区。活性区保持算符形式而非求迹为行列式标量权重，通过参考计算确定投影关联矩阵或转移矩阵，进而定义有限有效哈密顿量。有限密度依赖在投影后引入。

Result: 在三个基准测试中验证了方法：双通道谐振子检验条件基压缩；正测度随机计算检验关联矩阵提取与 GEVP 求解，并与 PDMS 估计器对比；四格点 Hubbard 环结合有限预算行列式符号应力测试与有限密度延拓。结果表明标量符号重新加权到受控活性空间近似的折中是可行的。

Conclusion: CEH 方法在结构有限模型中有效，但尚未提供端到端随机 CEH 处理 Hubbard 符号问题的完整方案，也未证明有利的可扩展性。其有效性依赖于低秩可逼近性和所需投影矩阵数据的高效提取。

Abstract: Euclidean Monte Carlo methods are effective when the path-integral weight is real and nonnegative, but finite-density fermion systems often produce sign-changing or complex scalar weights after the fermionic sector is traced out. Hamiltonian formulations avoid this complex-weight sampling problem but face rapid Hilbert-space growth. This paper studies a conditional Euclidean-Hamiltonian (CEH) reduction that combines these two descriptions.
  The calculation is organized around a Monte Carlo-tractable reference problem and a residual active sector. Instead of tracing the active sector into a determinant or scalar weight, CEH keeps it operator-valued and uses the reference calculation to determine projected correlation or transfer matrices. These matrices define a finite effective Hamiltonian, with the remaining finite-density dependence introduced after projection. At finite rank, the result is an effective model whose accuracy must be tested through basis enlargement, metric conditioning, stochastic matrix-element errors, and number-sector diagnostics.
  The construction is examined in three finite benchmarks. A two-channel oscillator tests conditional basis compression; a positive-measure stochastic calculation tests correlation-matrix harvesting and GEVP extraction, including a comparison with a PDMS-style estimator; and a four-site Hubbard ring combines a finite-budget determinant-sign stress test with finite-density continuation in non-target active spaces. The benchmarks support the proposed trade from scalar sign reweighting to a monitored active-space approximation in structured finite models, but they do not provide an end-to-end stochastic CEH treatment of the Hubbard sign problem or establish favorable scaling. Usefulness requires both low-rank approximability and efficient extraction of the required projected matrix data.

</details>


### [109] [Parameter Estimation in a Continuously Monitored Non-Markovian Quantum System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15978)
*Erik L. André,Pharnam Bakhshinezhad,Patrick P. Potts,Luis A. Correa,Mohammad Mehboudi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反应坐标映射的方法，将连续监测下的参数估计技术从马尔可夫动力系统扩展到非马尔可夫动力系统，适用于高斯连续测量下的线性系统，并给出了贝叶斯估计的解析表达式和费舍尔信息的渐近标度，通过玻色子热库测温实例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续监测在量子计量学中是一种重要的非破坏性技术，但现有的理论框架主要局限于马尔可夫动力学。将参数估计技术扩展到非马尔可夫动力学十分困难，因为这类动力学无法表达为完全正的可分映射。

Method: 基于反应坐标映射（reaction coordinate mapping）的方法，将非马尔可夫动力学转化为可处理的框架，针对经历高斯连续测量（如零差探测）的线性系统进行分析，推导贝叶斯估计的费舍尔信息解析表达式及估计精度的渐近标度。

Result: 给出了非马尔可夫动力学下高斯连续测量系统的贝叶斯估计解析表达式和费舍尔信息，以及任意参数估计精度的渐近标度；通过玻色子热库测温实例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将连续监测参数估计技术从马尔可夫系统扩展到非马尔可夫线性系统，为更广泛的量子计量场景提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring is a powerful tool for analyzing quantum systems and is increasingly used as a non-demolition technique in quantum metrology. In this context, noisy data acquired through continuous monitoring can be used to precisely pinpoint unknown parameters of the system. However, extending the theoretical framework that connects these data to the underlying parameters beyond Markovian dynamics is notoriously difficult, primarily because such dynamics cannot be expressed as completely positive divisible maps. To overcome this, we propose a method based on the reaction coordinate mapping to extend these parameter-estimation techniques beyond the Markovian regime. Our approach specifically targets linear systems with non-Markovian dynamics undergoing Gaussian continuous measurements, such as homodyne detection. Within this framework, we analyze Bayesian estimation and provide an analytical expression for the Fisher information, alongside the asymptotic scaling of the estimation precision for arbitrary parameters. Finally, we demonstrate the efficacy of our method through the example of thermometry of a bosonic bath.

</details>


### [110] [Tunable Families of Multiqubit Elegant Joint Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16020)
*Jef Pauwels,Nicolas Gisin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了n量子比特优雅联合测量(EJM)的闭式构造，证明其属于具有四面体布洛赫向量的可调测量族，并发现偶数n下可改变局部四面体大小（即基的纠缠度），遵循与已知两量子比特族相同的单参数规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于PRL 136, 190201 (2026)提出的EJM，作者希望给出其闭式构造，并进一步探索能否在保持对称性的同时改变局部四面体的大小（即基的纠缠度），从而推广到更一般的可调测量族。

Method: 利用初等对称函数构造的单相位多项式所隐含的干涉图案，构建EJM的闭式表达式；通过分析局部四面体大小的可调性，研究偶数n和奇数n情形下的族扩展可能性。

Result: 给出了对任意n有效的EJM闭式构造，对应Clifford层级n+1的测量酉算子。对于偶数n，可改变四面体大小并遵循单参数规律，可连续插值到1-均匀基；n=3时EJM局部孤立；n≥5奇数时未发现类似闭式族。此外还给出了对n≥3均有效的方形局部几何构造。

Conclusion: EJM可推广为具有可调纠缠度的测量族（偶数n），而奇数n情形则存在局限性，表明EJM的对称性与可调性依赖于量子比特数的奇偶性。

Abstract: We give a closed-form construction of the $n$-qubit Elegant Joint Measurement (EJM) proposed in [PRL \textbf{136}, 190201 (2026)] and show that it is part of a tunable family of measurements with tetrahedrally arranged Bloch vectors. The construction is based on the interference pattern implied by a single phase polynomial built from the elementary symmetric functions. It realises a regular tetrahedral measurement for every $n$, and the corresponding measurement unitary lies at level $n{+}1$ of the Clifford hierarchy. Starting from this measurement, we ask whether the size of the local tetrahedron -- and hence the entanglement of the basis -- can be varied while preserving its symmetry. For every even $n$ the answer is yes, and remarkably the size follows the same one-parameter law that governs the known two-qubit family, interpolating down to a $1$-uniform basis. For $n=3$ the EJM is locally isolated, while for odd $n\ge5$ we do not know an analogous closed-form family. We also give an analogous construction, valid for every $n \geq3$, with square local geometry.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Mechanics on Lie Groups: II. Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16029)
*Mathieu Beauvillain,Blagoje Oblak,Marios Petropoulos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在李群$G$的希尔伯特空间$L^2(G)$中构建了路径积分，通过处理非对易动量空间和紧致方向，将紧致性转化为极大环面上的绕数求和，并计算了Euler-Arnold系统的传播子和配分函数的半经典近似至两圈阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续arXiv:2512.19840对李群上量子动力学的研究，旨在构建支配跃迁振幅的路径积分，特别处理非对易动量空间和李群紧致方向带来的挑战。

Method: 通过将紧致性转化为极大环面上的绕数求和（推广了圆周上路径积分的绕数和），构建了$L^2(G)$上的路径积分；并应用半经典近似计算传播子和配分函数。

Result: 成功构建了李群上的路径积分框架，并将Euler-Arnold系统的传播子和配分函数的半经典近似计算推进至两圈阶。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效处理了李群紧致方向和非对易动量空间问题，绕数求和机制是处理紧致群路径积分的通用工具，可推广至更广泛的量子动力学系统。

Abstract: We continue our study of quantum dynamics on a Lie group $G$, initiated in arXiv:2512.19840, by building the path integral that governs transition amplitudes in the Hilbert space $L^2(G)$. This relies on the proper handling of both a noncommutative momentum space and the presence of compact directions in $G$. We show that compactness can be handled through a sum over winding numbers in maximal tori of $G$, generalizing the similar sum commonly encountered for path integrals on a circle. As applications, we compute semiclassical approximations of propagators and partition functions of Euler-Arnold systems, up to (and including) two-loop order.

</details>


### [112] [Rethinking Quantum Continual Learning with Quantum Fisher Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16030)
*Yu-Chao Hsu,Yu-Cheng Lin,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,En-Jui Kuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子弹性权重巩固（QEWC）方法，利用量子Fisher信息（QFI）替代经典Fisher信息（CFI）来度量参数重要性，从而缓解变分量子分类器在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。实验表明QEWC在经典图像分类和量子相位分类任务上有效，且对退极化噪声具有更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子分类器在非平稳任务分布下容易发生灾难性遗忘，传统基于经典Fisher信息（CFI）的弹性权重巩固（EWC）方法依赖于测量依赖的输出统计量，无法准确反映参数化量子态的内在敏感性。因此需要一种基于量子态几何结构的更本质的正则化方法。

Method: 提出量子弹性权重巩固（QEWC），使用量子Fisher信息（QFI）衡量参数化量子态对参数的敏感性，通过信息几何视角识别对量子态流形影响较大的重要参数，并以QFI为权重施加正则化约束，防止关键参数在训练新任务时发生大幅偏移。

Result: 在顺序二分类任务（经典图像分类和量子相位分类）上，无正则化训练导致严重遗忘，CFI-based EWC和QFI-based QEWC均能提升旧任务保留率。机理分析表明：CFI选择性作用于测量敏感方向，QFI施加更密集的态几何约束；在退极化噪声下，CFI因测量统计量退化而受到强烈抑制，QFI则保持更稳定的敏感性结构。

Conclusion: QEWC是一种基于量子态几何的物理启发式方法，为研究并缓解量子持续学习中的灾难性遗忘提供了有效途径，特别是在噪声环境下具有优势。

Abstract: Quantum continual learning aims to train quantum models on sequential tasks without losing previously learned knowledge. However, variational quantum classifiers (VQCs) are prone to catastrophic forgetting under nonstationary task distributions. We propose quantum elastic weight consolidation (QEWC), a quantum Fisher information (QFI)-informed regularization method for mitigating forgetting. Unlike conventional elastic weight consolidation based on classical Fisher information (CFI), which measures parameter importance through measurement-dependent output statistics, QEWC uses QFI to quantify the intrinsic sensitivity of the parameterized quantum state. This gives an information-geometric view in which important parameters are identified by the local response of the quantum state manifold. We evaluate QEWC on VQCs trained on sequential binary classification tasks, including classical image-classification and quantum phase-classification tasks. Simulations show that sequential training without regularization causes severe forgetting, while both CFI-based EWC and QFI-based QEWC improve retention of previous tasks. Mechanistic analyses further show that the two methods impose different regularization geometries: CFI acts selectively on measurement-sensitive directions, whereas QFI imposes a denser state-geometric constraint over parameter space. Under depolarizing noise, CFI values are strongly suppressed by degraded measurement statistics, while QFI preserves a more stable sensitivity structure of the noisy parameterized quantum state. These results establish QEWC as a physically motivated approach for studying and mitigating forgetting in quantum continual learning through quantum-state geometry.

</details>


### [113] [Rigorous Time-dependent Hamiltonian Learning via Continuous Weak Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16047)
*Jesús Jiménez-Rodríguez,Giacomo Franceschetto,Antonio Acín,Luciano Pereira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从连续弱测量记录中学习时变多体哈密顿量的严格且实验友好的协议，通过利用相互作用稀疏性将全局重构转化为局部逆问题，并给出了重构误差界和样本复杂度定理，在最多8量子比特的时变自旋链上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征量子处理器实际实现的哈密顿量对于校准和验证当前量子硬件至关重要，但许多器件在设计中就存在时间相关的生成器，现有方法难以有效处理时变哈密顿量的学习问题。

Method: 利用相互作用稀疏性将全局哈密顿量重构分解为一系列局部逆问题；使用纯可分离探针态驱动这些逆问题；通过图着色构造将局部探针嵌入少量全局乘积态制备；从连续弱测量记录中提取信息，并严格分离有限采样统计噪声与迭代状态更新的确定性偏差。

Result: 推导了显式的重构误差界和样本复杂度定理；在最多8量子比特的时变自旋链上验证了协议的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为从连续监测中进行时变哈密顿量学习奠定了严格的基础，建立的框架可自然扩展到多种平台和探针系综。

Abstract: Characterizing the Hamiltonian that a quantum processor actually implements is central to calibrating and validating current quantum hardware. Many devices, however, operate with generators that are time dependent by design. Here we develop a rigorous and experimentally friendly protocol for learning time-dependent many-body Hamiltonians from continuous weak measurement records. The key observation is that interaction sparsity reduces the global reconstruction to a set of local inverse problems, whose number is controlled by the interaction connectivity rather than by the system size. Pure separable probe states suffice to drive these inversions, and a graph-coloring construction embeds them into a small number of global product-state preparations. We derive explicit reconstruction-error bounds and a sample-complexity theorem that cleanly separates the finite-sampling statistical noise from the deterministic bias of the iterative state update, and we validate the protocol on time-dependent spin chains with up to $n=8$ qubits. Beyond these results, our analysis provides a rigorous foundation for time-dependent Hamiltonian learning from continuous monitoring in many-body systems, establishing a framework that extends naturally to many platforms and probe ensembles.

</details>


### [114] [Operator Entanglement in Quantum Dynamics Simulations: Formalism and Analysis Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16070)
*Tzu Yu Wang,Michael Schuurman,Simon Neville*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了超约化密度矩阵（SRDM）和超互信息（SMI）框架，用于分析振动和振动耦合哈密顿量。通过自然单粒子算符基实现高效压缩，并利用SMI揭示直接和间接耦合。数值实验表明该方法可在不损失精度的情况下实现高压缩比，并能系统性量化包括通过电子-振动相互作用产生的间接模式耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的振动和振动耦合哈密顿量维度极高，计算成本巨大；同时，模式间的直接和间接耦合（尤其是通过电子-振动相互作用产生的间接耦合）难以从原始哈密顿量中直接提取和量化。

Method: 引入算符希尔伯特空间框架，定义1-SRDM和2-SRDM，其本征向量定义自然单粒子算符基以实现受控误差压缩；基于1-SRDM和2-SRDM的算符纠缠熵定义SMI，并开发高效数值计算方法。

Result: (i) 常用振动耦合哈密顿量可在不损失精度条件下实现极高压缩比；(ii) SMI分析能系统性、定量地揭示直接和间接耦合，包括通过中间电子-振动相互作用产生的振动模式间接耦合。

Conclusion: SRDM和SMI框架为振动和振动耦合系统的哈密顿量压缩与耦合分析提供了有效的理论工具，可推广到更复杂的量子系统。

Abstract: We review the framework of operator Hilbert space and introduce the one- and two-particle super reduced density matrices (1-SRDMs and 2-SRDMs), as well as the super mutual information (SMI). The eigenvectors of the 1-SRDMs define what we term natural single particle operator bases, and provide a way to compress vibrational and vibronic Hamiltonians with controlled error. The SMI is defined from the operator entanglement entropy of the 1-SRDMs and 2-SRDMs, and captures the correlation between operators acting on different one-mode subspaces, which may be used to reveal and quantify both direct and indirect couplings that might otherwise be difficult to extract. Efficient numerical approaches for the calculation of SRDMs and the SMI are developed and applied to a set of prototypical vibrational and vibronic Hamiltonians, as well as approximations to the corresponding time-evolution operators. Through this, we demonstrate that: (i) commonly used vibronic Hamiltonians are amenable to extremely high levels of compression without compromising accuracy, and; (ii) SMI analysis can be used to systematically and quantitatively reveal both direct and indirect couplings that might otherwise be difficult to extract, including indirect couplings of vibrational modes via intermediary electronic-vibrational interactions.

</details>


### [115] [Object-relative ultraviolet weighting of electromagnetic modes and one-loop ultraviolet finiteness in quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16096)
*Christian Rembe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局域化电磁相互作用的有效紫外加权方案，通过物体相对坐标系下的协变模频率来抑制高频贡献，使单圈QED贡献变得紫外有限，并在反常磁矩、兰姆位移、卡西米尔效应等测试案例中得到物理合理的特征尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索局域化电磁相互作用能否通过有效的、相对于相互作用物体的紫外加权方案来建模，以解决标准量子电动力学中紫外发散问题，并检验不同观测量的局域化尺度。

Method: 基于弱场自反作用估计和三维重叠论证构建启发式模型，提出以物体相对协变模变量 $u_μ k^μ$（即局域化相互作用物体静止系中的模频率）为加权依据，抑制紫外贡献（渐近因子 $k_c^3/k^3$），红外区域不变。将该加权方案一致应用于自能和顶点修正中的内部光子模。

Result: 在选定的单圈QED贡献中，紫外发散被消除；受限的单圈Ward恒等式一致性检验得以保留；四个测试案例（反常磁矩、Bethe型低能兰姆位移、卡西米尔效应、单圈紫外测试）均给出物理合理的特征尺度（分别对应电子康普顿尺度、原子束缚态尺度、极板间距）。

Conclusion: 不同观测量可能探测不同的有效局域化尺度；该方案在物理直觉上是合理的，但作用量层面的推导、谱一致性和超越单圈的推广仍是开放问题。

Abstract: This work explores whether localized electromagnetic interactions can be modeled in terms of an effective object-relative ultraviolet weighting of internal modes. The proposal is motivated heuristically by two considerations: a weak-field self-backreaction estimate for sufficiently localized energy-carrying modes and a three-dimensional overlap argument for localized interactions. In the resulting ansatz, the infrared sector remains unchanged up to a characteristic scale the at angular wavenumber $k_c$, while ultraviolet contributions are suppressed asymptotically by a factor of order $k_c^3/k^3$ with the angular wavenumber $k$. Because a crossover based solely on $k^μk_μ$ is not well suited to the intended mode-based interpretation, the weighting is formulated in terms of the object-relative covariant mode variable $u_μ k^μ$ with the four-velocity $u^μ$, i.e. the mode frequency measured in the rest frame of the localized interaction object. Within this restricted framework, selected one-loop QED contributions considered here become ultraviolet finite, and a restricted one-loop Ward-consistency check is preserved when the same scalar weighting is assigned consistently to the same internal photon mode in self-energy and vertex corrections. Four initial test cases are discussed: the anomalous magnetic moment, a Bethe-type low-energy Lamb-shift estimate, the Casimir effect, and a compact ultraviolet one-loop test. In the first three cases, the weighting leads to physically sensible characteristic scales associated with the electron Compton scale, an atomic bound-state scale, and plate distance, respectively. The results suggest that different observables may probe different effective localization scales. Action-level derivation, spectral consistency, and extension beyond one loop remain open problems.

</details>


### [116] [Parameterized Quantum Circuit Semantics Through Enriched Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16114)
*Neil J. Ross,Scott Wesley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用丰富范畴论为参数化量子电路建立语义框架，解决了传统幺半群范畴模型无法描述参数化电路族的问题，并统一了量子控制的两种视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的组合电路建模使用幺半范畴中的弦图，量子电路语义通过幺半函子映射到酉矩阵范畴。但该模型无法描述参数化量子电路族（如变分量子算法中的ansatz电路），因此需要更通用的语义框架。

Method: 基于丰富范畴论（enriched category theory）引入抽象的范畴构造，分别研究笛卡尔幺半参数和幺半闭参数两种特殊情况，赋予参数化语义以有用的结构性质。

Result: 该方法能够描述参数化电路族，为受控操作和量子通信提供了新的见解，并在幺半闭参数情形下统一了量子控制的两种不同视角。

Conclusion: 丰富范畴论为参数化量子电路提供了一种统一的、具有良好结构性质的语义框架，不仅解决了现有模型的局限性，还为理解量子控制提供了更深层的范畴论基础。

Abstract: It is well-known that combinatorial circuits are modeled mathematically by string diagrams in monoidal categories. Given a gate set $Σ$, the circuits over $Σ$ can be thought of as string diagrams in the free monoidal category generated by $Σ$. In this model, circuit semantics are then given by monoidal functors out of this free category. For quantum circuits, this functor is often valued in the category of unitary matrices. This model suffices for concrete quantum circuits, but fails to describe parameterized families of quantum circuits, such as those which arise in the analysis of ansatz circuits. In this paper, we introduce an approach to parameterized circuit semantics, which is based on enriched category theory. We first introduce an abstract categorical construction, and use this to gain new insights on controlled operations and quantum communication. We then study the special cases of Cartesian monoidal parameters and monoidal closed parameters, both endowing the parameterized semantics with useful constructions.We conclude by showing that the monoidal closed case can be used to unify two perspectives on quantum control.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum-classical crossover in fault-tolerant quantum dynamics simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16116)
*Jinzhao Sun,Bozhen Zhou,Jue Xu,Yuan Yao,Zhenyu Du,Zixu Zhang,Yuntian Gu,Junxiang Huang,Shuo Zhou,Ziruo Wang,Alexander Yosifov,Wenzheng Dong,Yiming Huang,Daniel Serrano,Xinzhao Wang,Tianfeng Feng,Shreyas Sadugol,Wenjun Yu,Zhou You,Dayue Qin,Xiao-Ming Zhang,Yantao Wu,Aditya Iyer,You Zhou,Tongyang Li,Ying Li,Xiongfeng Ma,Qi Zhao,Pei Zeng,Pan Zhang,Xiao Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了容错量子计算在量子多体动力学模拟中超越经典算法的具体交叉点。在物理错误率10^{-3}下，100位一维系统需2小时和37万物理量子比特，而张量网络需约100年；二维系统量子运行时间仅数分钟。错误率降至10^{-4}时，量子资源进一步大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定容错量子计算在实用问题上超越最优经典算法的具体临界点仍是悬而未决的挑战。

Method: 提出可扩展容错框架，结合相干可观测量估计与时空高效的非Clifford旋转实现，抑制部分容错方法中的残余逻辑错误；通过改进旋转态注入以及量子纠错与可观测量估计协议的协同设计降低逻辑错误累积和采样开销。

Result: 对于混合场Ising动力学：p=10^{-3}时，1D 100位系统需2小时/3.7×10^5物理量子比特（经典需100年），2D仅数分钟；p=10^{-4}时，量子比特降至3.1×10^4，1D数分钟，2D数秒。

Conclusion: 建立了实现实用量子优势的可扩展路径，并为未来容错架构提供了量化工程目标。

Abstract: While quantum computers promise to solve classically intractable problems, identifying the point at which fault-tolerant quantum computation outperforms the best classical algorithms for practical applications remains an outstanding challenge. Here we establish a concrete quantum-classical crossover for quantum many-body dynamics under realistic hardware conditions. We introduce a scalable fault-tolerant framework that combines coherent observable estimation with a space-time-efficient implementation of non-Clifford rotations, suppressing the residual logical errors that limit existing partially fault-tolerant approaches. A benchmark against state-of-the-art tensor-network and variational Monte Carlo algorithms reveals a concrete crossover for mixed-field Ising dynamics at modest system sizes. For a physical error rate of $p=10^{-3}$, fault-tolerant simulation requires approximately 2 hours and $3.7 \times 10^5$ physical qubits for a 100-site 1D system, whereas tensor network approaches would require about 100 years. For 2D models, where rapid entanglement growth limits the classical evolution time, we project quantum runtimes within minutes. A physical error rate of $p=10^{-4}$ leads to at least an order of magnitude reduction in qubit count ($3.1 \times 10^4$ physical qubits) and runtime (minutes for 1D and seconds for 2D). The reduction in quantum runtime arises from our improved rotation-state injection and co-design of quantum error correction and observable-estimation protocols, which jointly suppress logical-error accumulation and reduce sampling overhead. Our results establish a scalable route towards practical quantum advantage and identify quantitative engineering targets for future fault-tolerant architectures.

</details>


### [118] [Reverse engineering of single-qubit quantum gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16124)
*Gustavo Fernandes da Costa,Leonardo K. Castelano,Emanuel Fernandes de Lima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用线偏振调制正弦场实现任意单量子比特门（SU(2)矩阵），在旋转波近似下通过逆向求解运动方程得到控制场的解析公式，并引入可调自由参数优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决利用线偏振场设计单量子比特量子门的问题，提供一种通用且解析的控制场构造方法。

Method: 在旋转波近似下，逆向求解演化算符的运动方程，将期望的幺正门条件代入，得到调制正弦控制场的解析表达式；引入可自由调节的动力学函数参数以优化性能指标。

Result: 任意SU(2)单量子比特门均可由线偏振调制正弦场生成，且控制场具有闭合解析形式，包含可调自由参数。

Conclusion: 该方法为单量子比特门实现提供了一种仅依赖旋转波近似的解析控制方案，且可通过自由参数灵活调整以满足不同性能需求。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of designing single-qubit quantum gates by means of a linearly-polarized field. We show that any desired one-qubit gate corresponding to a special unitary matrix can be generated by a modulated sinusoidal field. The only approximation involved is the rotating-wave-approximation. The formula for the control field is obtained by inverting the equation of motion for the evolution operator and imposing the conditions for the desired gate. We give a simple procedure to obtain closed analytical formulas for the fields in terms of a priori chosen dynamical functions. Additionally, these dynamical functions can depend on tunable free parameters intentionally introduced to meet a desired performance criteria.

</details>


### [119] [Fast logical operations in quantum LDPC codes using simple resource states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16166)
*Mark Webster,Nicolas Delfosse*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于猫态的高效量子LDPC码逻辑测量协议，通过设计调度器编码实现多个对易逻辑算子的联合测量，在Q70和Q102码上相比Viterbi测量获得近3倍加速，结合改进的CliNR纠错方案后对随机Clifford电路加速达74倍，对Toffoli门加速达5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码虽然以其高编码率大幅降低了容错量子计算所需的量子比特开销，但在同一编码块中同时对多个逻辑量子比特进行操作更为困难，会拖慢逻辑运算速度。现有工作通过设计复杂资源态来解决LDPC码中的逻辑测量问题，但复杂度较高。本文旨在提出一种仅消耗猫态的简化方案来解决多逻辑算子联合测量的高效实现问题。

Method: 提出一种仅使用猫态（cat states）的协议，核心创新在于设计了一个"调度器编码"（scheduler code），用于确定测量序列并解码所有逻辑测量结果。该协议支持对ℓ个对易逻辑算子进行联合测量，而非仅单个逻辑测量。此外，还结合了一种新的CliNR部分纠错方案变体来提升整体性能。

Result: 在walking cat架构的LDPC码Q70和Q102上进行的数值仿真表明：对于ℓ=20个对易逻辑算子的测量，相比Viterbi测量方法获得近3倍加速；结合改进的CliNR部分纠错方案后，随机Clifford电路加速达74倍；对于非Clifford门的Toffoli门，加速达5倍。

Conclusion: 本文的协议有效解决了LDPC码中多逻辑算子联合测量的瓶颈问题，通过调度器编码的设计实现了仅依赖猫态的高效逻辑测量，在多种场景下均取得了显著的加速效果，为高编码率LDPC码在容错量子计算中的实际应用提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Quantum LPDC codes provide a substantial reduction in qubit overhead required for fault-tolerant quantum computation compared to surface code, thanks to their high encoding rate. However, operating simultaneously on multiple logical qubits encoded in the same block is more challenging and may slow down logical operations. Prior work addresses this problem by designing complex resource states to perform logical measurements in LDPC codes. Here, we propose an approach that only consumes cat states. Whereas previous work on cat-based measurements focuses on a single logical measurement, we design a protocol for the joint measurement of $\ell$ commuting logical operators. The key ingredient is the design of a scheduler code determining the measurement sequence and allowing for the decoding of all logical measurement outcomes. Numerical simulations with the LDPC codes Q70 and Q102 of the walking cat architecture show a speed-up of nearly $3\times$ over Viterbi measurements for the measurement of $\ell=20$ commuting logical operators. Combining our fast logical measurements with a new variant of the CliNR partial error correction scheme, we achieve a speed up of up to up to $74\times$ for random Clifford circuits. Our approach also applies to non-Clifford gates, producing a speed up of up to $5\times$ for Toffoli gates.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate scalar quasinormal modes and ringdown signals of a rotating loop quantum gravity black hole constructed via the revised Newman--Janis algorithm. The spacetime incorporates two quantum parameters, the polymeric parameter $P$ and the minimal area parameter $a_0$, and reduces to the Kerr spacetime in the classical limit. Using the sixth-order WKB approximation and time-domain integration, we analyze the effective potential, quasinormal mode spectrum, and ringdown dynamics. The polymeric parameter $P$ systematically reduces the oscillation frequency $\mathrm{Re}(ω)$, while the spin parameter $a$ enhances it. The time-domain signals exhibit clear dependence on the multipole number $\ell$ and overtone index $n$. Our results provide a theoretical framework for understanding the effects of loop quantum gravity corrections on black hole perturbations and gravitational-wave ringdown signals.

</details>

Abstract: We study a class of Schwarzschild black holes embedded in an exponential-spheroidal dark matter halo, modelled by a phenomenological density profile $ρ(r)=ρ_0 e^{-r/r_0}$. By solving the Einstein equations for a static, spherically symmetric spacetime, we obtain an analytic solution for the lapse function that reduces to the Schwarzschild spacetime in the absence of the halo and to a regular halo configuration when the central black hole mass vanishes. Indeed, the two halo parameters, $ρ_0$ and $r_0$, describe the strength and radial extent of the dark matter distribution. We analyse the curvature structure, energy conditions, shadow observables, scalar quasi-normal modes and grey-body bounds of the resulting spacetime. The Ricci scalar and the Ricci square remain finite at the origin, whilst the Kretschmann scalar retains the usual central tidal singularity in the presence of a black hole mass. The weak, null, and dominant energy conditions are satisfied, whilst the strong energy condition is violated on a finite radial interval. We also show that the halo monotonically shifts the photon sphere and the shadow radius, which allows us to derive approximate constraints from the EHT observations of M87* and Sgr A*. For scalar perturbations, the Padé-resummed WKB approximation yields stable quasinormal frequencies, while the standard WKB approximation becomes unreliable for higher overtones and strong-halo configurations. Finally, the greybody bounds indicate that the halo weakens transmission through the effective barrier, particularly at low frequencies.

</details>

Method: 采用扩展Dirac-Bergmann嵌入和Faddeev-Jackiw约化两种独立方法，识别第一类约束并提取约化泊松括号；直接从约化Dirac-Bergmann第一类约束理想恢复Casimir；在静态扇区进行规范固定并验证场方程。

Abstract: Hamiltonian analysis of the first-order Katanaev-Volovich model of two-dimensional gravity with torsion. The first-order action already singles out natural canonical pairs: the auxiliary Lorentz scalars are conjugate to the spatial connection and zweibein. An extended Dirac-Bergmann embedding isolates an auxiliary second-class sector whose elimination recovers the canonical structure encoded in the first-order action and leaves the first-class constraints generating the local gauge symmetries. An independent Faddeev-Jackiw reduction yields the same reduced brackets. The Katanaev/Poisson-Sigma Casimir is then recovered, up to normalization, directly from the reduced Dirac-Bergmann first-class constraint ideal, before imposing any gauge condition. This identifies the Casimir as a global label of the reduced canonical sectors; after the static normalization is chosen, it supplies the Hamiltonian parameter of the torsionful branch. The same normalization is applied to that branch in dilaton gauge, whose field equations are verified on shell. In this static sector the Casimir-normalized radial field does not coincide with the metric Killing norm: the diagonal representative satisfies \(NB=e^{4βr}\). Consequently, torsion modifies the Killing temperature through the normalization of the Killing time, while the horizon entropy retains its standard two-dimensional dilaton value and the Casimir-normalized first law holds.

</details>

Result: （1）辐射效率η严格与α无关，但随l和ρ₀变化显著；（2）j=0.4, l=0.5M_BH, ρ₀=2×10⁻⁴时η=8.71%，高于Kerr的7.51%；（3）粘滞修正比δℱ/ℱ_NTP在ISCO处发散，受几何边界压力放大；（4）粘滞放大比从Kerr的0.80%-4.52%提升至Hayward+DE的0.87%-6.76%（α=0.1）。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了独立于光谱归一化的单调观测判别器，可用于NICER和NuSTAR等望远镜区分不同黑洞几何；同时建立了一种通过结合吸积盘观测打破Hayward尺度与暗能量密度参数简并的理论策略。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive relativistic framework for geometrically thin, optically thick Shakura--Sunyaev $α$-viscous accretion disks around rotating Hayward regular black holes embedded in a quintessence dark energy (DE) field. The static, spherically symmetric building blocks of our spacetime are each exact solutions of the Einstein field equations, sourced respectively by the Hayward non-linear electromagnetic field and a quintessence fluid with equation-of-state parameter $ω<-1/3$; we combine and rotate them following standard practice for this class of models, and we are explicit throughout about the resulting metric's phenomenological status. Abandoning the stress-free inner boundary of the Novikov--Thorne--Page (NTP) model, we analytically incorporate a non-zero viscous torque $\Tcal_{\rm in}$ at the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) via the relativistic vertical epicyclic frequency $\Obot(r)$. We prove that the bolometric efficiency $η=[1-E(\rISCO)]\times100\,\%$ is strictly independent of the viscosity parameter $α$ but sensitive to both the Hayward length scale $l$ and the DE density $ρ_0$, establishing a rigorous two-observable degeneracy-breaking strategy. At benchmark parameters ($j=0.4$, $l=0.5M_{\BH}$, $ρ_0=2\times10^{-4}$), the combined geometry yields $η=8.71\,\%$, substantially above the vacuum Kerr value $7.51\,\%$ at the same spin. The viscosity correction $δ\Fcal/\Fcal_{\rm NTP}$ diverges at $r\to\rISCO^+$, amplified by the geometric odification of the boundary pressure: the viscosity amplification ratio rises from $0.80$--$4.52\,\%$ (vacuum Kerr) to $0.87$--$6.76\,\%$ (Hayward+DE) at $α=0.1$, providing a clean, monotonic observational discriminator accessible to \textit{NICER} and \textit{NuSTAR}, independent of the bulk spectral normalisation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 标准Dirichlet条件下复现了已知标量谱；对于每个非Dirichlet值，在小、中、大黑洞的代表性情况下都发现了一个具有正虚部的额外模式，表明存在边界条件诱导的不稳定性。近Dirichlet细化分析表明，即使在最小形变下也未发现有限临界角。

Conclusion: 广义AdS边界条件会破坏标准拟正规模谱的稳定性，引入不稳定的额外模式，且这种不稳定性在任意小的边界条件形变下都存在。

Abstract: We study quasinormal modes (QNMs) of a minimally coupled massless scalar field on four-dimensional Schwarzschild--anti-de Sitter black holes using a Chebyshev spectral method. After compactifying the exterior domain, the radial problem is formulated as a quadratic matrix pencil in the dimensionless frequency. For the standard Dirichlet, or vanishing-field, boundary condition at the conformal AdS boundary, we reproduce the known scalar spectra across small, intermediate, and large black holes, including long overtone sequences and the expected approach to pure-AdS normal modes in the small black hole limit. We then deform the AdS boundary condition by imposing a generalized relation between the two independent asymptotic coefficients of the massless scalar. This deformation is treated as a generalized coefficient boundary condition for the massless scalar, and not as the usual alternative quantization for scalars in the Breitenlohner-Freedman window. The Dirichlet endpoint recovers the stable standard spectrum. For every non-Dirichlet value examined, and for representative small, intermediate, and large black holes, we find an additional mode with positive imaginary part, signaling a boundary-condition-induced instability. A near-Dirichlet refinement finds no finite critical angle down to the smallest deformation probed.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the behavior of Einstein crystals in noncommutative backgrounds described by the Snyder and Snyder-de Sitter models. Possible novel effects, which may arise in realistic systems such as diamond crystals, are analyzed within a thermodynamical framework. We show that noncommutativity influences the key thermodynamic quantities, including internal energy and specific heat. These corrections can be directly related to modifications of the underlying uncertainty relations, of the generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) and generalized extended uncertainty principle (GEUP) types.

</details>

Abstract: We study the gravitational interaction of very strong pulses of electromagnetic radiation, up to and beyond gravitational collapse. We demonstrate the existence of very compact states close to the threshold of collapse, both for a single pulse, and a head-on collision of such pulses, and we measure their compactness to be above the maximum value proposed by the hoop conjecture. For a single pulse above the threshold of collapse, a fast black hole is produced. Depending on the amount of tuning, head-on collisions can form two horizonless states, two small black holes or one big black hole. Our results show that the gravitational interaction of massless fields can lead to nontrivial final or intermediate states, while giving rise to extremely high luminosity and compactness.

*Behzad Eslam Panah,Abdullah Guvendi,Semra Gurtas Dogan,Omar Mustafa*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自发洛伦兹对称性破缺在蜂鸟引力框架下对AdS黑洞的标量波传播、几何光学极限及热力学性质的影响，建立洛伦兹破缺与黑洞物理之间的关联。

Method: 采用静态球对称蜂鸟黑洞解，通过径向Klein-Gordon方程化为广义Helmholtz形式推导有效折射率；分析高频极限下波传播与零测地线的对应关系；在非扩展和扩展相空间中验证热力学第一定律和Smarr关系，并构建热机循环计算效率。

Result: 洛伦兹破缺参数ℓ控制径向缩放及几何光学极限；AdS边界反射波而视界单向吸收；第一定律和Smarr关系成立；热容量、自由能和稳定性受ℓ影响；热机效率随ℓ增加并存在η≤1的上界。

Conclusion: 建立了洛伦兹破缺、波传播、几何光学与AdS黑洞热力学之间的统一框架，揭示ℓ是调控上述过程的关键参数。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of spontaneous Lorentz symmetry breaking on scalar wave propagation, null geodesics, and thermodynamic behavior of four-dimensional asymptotically AdS black holes in bumblebee gravity. The static, spherically symmetric solutions are characterized by a dimensionless parameter $\ell > -1$ arising from the vacuum expectation value of the bumblebee vector field, which globally rescales the radial geometry. Massless scalar fields are analyzed via the radial Klein--Gordon equation cast into a generalized Helmholtz form, yielding an effective frequency-dependent refractive index that identifies oscillatory and evanescent regions, classical turning points, and confinement induced by curvature and Lorentz violation. In the high-frequency limit, wave propagation coincides with null geodesics, with $\ell$ controlling radial scaling and governing the geometric-optics limit. The AdS boundary reflects waves, while the horizon acts as a one-way absorber. Thermodynamic analysis in non-extended and extended phase spaces confirms the first law and Smarr relation, with $\ell$ influencing heat capacity, free energy, and stability. \textcolor{black}{Modeling these black holes as heat engines, we construct explicit cycles and show that efficiency increases with $\ell$, leading to an upper bound imposed by $η\leq 1$. Our results provide a framework connecting Lorentz violation, wave propagation, geometric optics, and AdS black hole thermodynamics in bumblebee gravity.

</details>


### [128] [On dark sector scalar field theories driven by cosmological $c$-fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15897)
*Alex E. Bernardini,Orfeu Bertolami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hoyle-Narlikar创生场宇宙学与暗区标量场模型（包括广义Chaplygin气体）之间的内在联系。通过拉格朗日标量场形式和哈密顿重构方法，证明HN c场动力学可被GCG状态方程所包含，形成统一描述。该框架可实现晚期宇宙加速和线性扰动稳定性，表明创生场宇宙学可嵌入更广泛的标量场模型中，微妙修正哈勃膨胀率及相关可观测物理量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Hoyle-Narlikar创生场宇宙学和广义Chaplygin气体源于不同的理论框架，但二者都涉及标量自由度，能够解决标准ΛCDM模型的一些局限性。作者旨在探索这两种理论之间的深层联系，寻找统一的描述框架。

Method: 采用拉格朗日标量场形式化方法，结合一阶哈密顿重构技术，将HN c场动力学等价转化为GCG状态方程所描述的修正标量场理论。

Result: HN创生场宇宙学可以被GCG通过等价的修正标量场理论所包含；该统一描述能够实现晚期宇宙加速和线性扰动的稳定性；创生场宇宙学可嵌入更广泛的标量场模型中，对哈勃膨胀率及相关物理可观测量产生微妙修正。

Conclusion: 本文建立了HN创生场宇宙学与广义Chaplygin气体之间的理论等价性，为暗能量和暗区物理提供了统一的理论框架，暗示了创生场模型可能属于更广泛的标量场模型家族。

Abstract: The interplay of Hoyle-Narlikar (HN) creation field cosmology and scalar field models for the dark sector, including the generalized Chaplygin Gas (GCG), is investigated . Though originating from distinct theoretical frameworks, both the inclusion and the non-inclusion of the creation field degree of freedom (DoF) involve a scalar DoF, which addresses some of the limitations of the standard $Λ$CDM model. Using a Lagrangian scalar field formulation and the first-order Hamiltonian reconstruction method, the HN $c$-field dynamics is shown to be encompassed by the GCG equation of state through an equivalent modified scalar field theory. Late-time acceleration and stability of linear perturbations are derived within this unified description. Our results suggest that creation field cosmologies may be embedded in a broader class of scalar field models which encompasses subtle modifications to the Hubble expansion rate and related physical observables.

</details>


### [129] [Accelerating electrically charged ModMax black hole solutions in $F(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15914)
*B. Eslam Panah,N. Heidari,A. Rincón*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在$F(R)$引力与ModMax非线性电磁场耦合的理论框架下，导出了四维加速黑洞的精确解，分析了参数对黑洞行为的影响，计算了Hawking温度和熵，并通过热容评估了局部稳定性，还研究了黑洞的角阴影和阴影半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在$F(R)$引力与ModMax非线性电磁场耦合的理论框架下，推导四维时空中加速黑洞的精确解，并系统研究各参数对黑洞热力学和阴影观测性质的影响。

Method: 利用$C-$度量在$F(R)-$ModMax理论中推导四维加速黑洞精确解；通过计算Hawking温度、熵和热容进行热力学分析；通过分析角阴影和阴影半径进行光学观测研究。

Result: 获得了$F(R)-$ModMax理论中加速黑洞的精确解；揭示了各参数对Hawking温度、熵的影响规律；通过热容分析确定了局部稳定性条件；得到了角阴影和阴影半径随参数变化的特征。

Conclusion: $F(R)-$ModMax理论中的加速黑洞具有丰富的热力学和光学性质，各理论参数对黑洞的稳定性和可观测阴影特征有显著影响。

Abstract: Using the $C-$metric in the context of $F(R)$ gravity coupled with the ModMax nonlinear electromagnetic field (the $F(R)-$ModMax theory), we derive an exact black hole solution in a four-dimensional spacetime. We then examine how various parameters influence the behavior of accelerating ModMax black holes. Treating this black hole as a thermodynamic system, we calculate the Hawking temperature and entropy for the accelerating ModMax black holes within the framework of $F(R)$ gravity. Subsequently, we explore the impact of the parameters in $F(R)-$ModMax theory on the Hawking temperature and entropy. We also assess local stability by analyzing the heat capacity. Additionally, we investigate both the angular shadow and the shadow radius of an accelerating black hole in the context of $F(R)-$ModMax gravity.

</details>


### [130] [Massless scalar scattering by Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black holes:transparent-end channels and superradiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16068)
*Hai Huang,Xudong Sun,Juhua Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在旋转Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞几何中，中性无质量标量场的实频散射问题。采用透明边界条件后，发现反射数据依赖于边界选择而非定义唯一的观测者无关截面。超辐射由双重门机制控制，且随着外磁场增加，共转偶极放大减弱，并在BM≈0.243处关闭超辐射窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Kerr-BR黑洞（带外磁场的旋转黑洞）上的标量场散射与超辐射特性，理解边界条件对散射数据的影响以及外磁场对超辐射的调控作用。

Method: 1. 采用透明边界条件数值求解实频散射问题；2. 利用无迹麦克斯韦应力张量将波动方程约化为共形波动方程，通过共形因子标度实现类Carter分离；3. 分析反射系数的边界条件依赖性；4. 研究超辐射的双重门机制（局部视界条件+外传播条件）。

Result: 1. 反射数据依赖于边界条件，非观测者无关；2. 超辐射需同时满足局部视界条件和外传播条件q_∞²>0；3. 在a/M=0.9时，共转偶极放大随外磁场B增大而减弱；4. 超辐射窗口在BM≈0.243处关闭；5. 近传播阈值处放大系数与外波数线性消失。

Conclusion: Kerr-BR黑洞上的超辐射受边界条件和外磁场强烈影响，外磁场可有效抑制甚至关闭超辐射窗口，这为理解磁化黑洞环境中的能量提取机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: We formulate and numerically solve the real-frequency scattering problem for a neutral, minimally coupled, massless scalar on the rotating Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson (Kerr--BR) black-hole geometry, using a specified transparent boundary condition at the coordinate end. Since the Maxwell stress tensor of the background is traceless, the minimally coupled wave equation reduces to the four-dimensional conformal wave equation, enabling exact Carter-like separation after scaling the scalar field by the conformal factor. Unlike the asymptotically flat case, the coordinate end $r\to\infty$ lies at a finite tortoise distance. We show that the resulting reflection data are conditional on this boundary prescription rather than defining a unique, observer-independent cross section. Within the transparent-end model,open-channel superradiance is governed by a double-gate mechanism requiring both the local horizon condition and the outer propagation condition $q_\infty^2>0$. At the benchmark spin $a/M=0.9$, the co-rotating dipole amplification decreases as the external magnetic field increases. Crucially, the field narrows and closes the open superradiant window at $BM\simeq0.243$. Near the propagation threshold, the amplification coefficient vanishes linearly with the outer wave number.

</details>


### [131] [Comparison of $f(R,T)$ Gravity with Multiple Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16158)
*Vincent R. Siggia,Eric D. Carlson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(R,T)=R+λT^ε修正引力模型，通过宇宙微波背景、重子声学振荡、宇宙计时器和Ia型超新星的数据分析，发现最佳拟合参数ε=0.010^{+0.013}_{-0.021}，与标准宇宙学模型(ε=0)一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验f(R,T)修正引力模型的有效性，特别是R+λT^ε形式，通过与标准ΛCDM模型的比较来验证其可行性和参数约束。

Method: 利用宇宙微波背景(CMB)、重子声学振荡(BAO)、宇宙计时器(CC)和Ia型超新星(SNe Ia)等多类观测数据，对f(R,T)=R+λT^ε引力模型进行参数拟合，并引入相关性和辐射效应进行改进分析。

Result: 最佳拟合参数ε=0.010^{+0.013}_{-0.021}，与标准宇宙学模型的ε=0值在误差范围内一致。

Conclusion: R+λT^ε修正引力模型的最佳拟合参数与ΛCDM标准模型无显著差异，表明当前观测数据不支持对该模型的显著偏离。

Abstract: We examine $f(R,T)=R+λT^ε$ gravity models by finding the best fit parameters for various values of $ε$. This is accomplished by analyzing data from the cosmic microwave background, baryon acoustic oscillation observations, cosmic chronometer, and Type Ia supernovae introducing correlations and radiation effects to our previous work. We find the probability distribution for the best fit model around the value of $ε=0.010^{+0.013}_{-0.021}$, consistent with the standard cosmological model value of $ε= 0$.

</details>
